Ingen familiekanter ændres.","provenance":"Direkte kontrol af det publicerede G298 skifteuddrag på milloup.dk, 17.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-17"},{"title":"DAISY identificerer herredsfogedens skifte- og auktionsserier efter negativ G298-kontrol","text":"Rigsarkivets DAISY-registratur for Djurs Sønder-Mols Herredsfoged (1687–1919) viser følgende relevante serier: “Navneregister til skifteprotokol” 1794–1917; “Skiftedesignationer og dødsanmeldelser” 1795–1851; “Skifteprotokol, Sønder Djurs og Mols Herred” 1794–1824; “Auktionsprotokol” 1792–1814 og 1753–1928; “Auktionsprotokol for by og herreder” 1753–1928; “Sager til auktionsprotokol” og “Dokumenter til auktionsprotokol” 1791–1928.\n\nDette er et konkret nyt spor efter at det publicerede G298-godsuddrag ikke viste Claus Gaardsted. Claus' bo kan være anmeldt/skiftet ved herredsfogeden, og enken kan have solgt Bogensholm ved en herredsauktion. Start med navneregistret til skifteprotokollen og skiftedesignationerne/dødsanmeldelserne omkring 1811; følg derefter henvisninger til original skifteprotokol. Søg auktionen separat i auktionsprotokollen og sagerne til auktionsprotokollen. Kataloget identificerer arkivserierne, men ingen af originalprotokollerne er endnu læst.\n\nKilde: Rigsarkivet DAISY, Djurs Sønder-Mols Herredsfoged, arkivserier: https://daisy.rigsarkivet.dk/arkivskabers_arkivserier?a=&b=&c=Djurs+S%C3%B8nde+herred&d=1&dir=&e=2022&ed=&ep=&epid=&es=&f=&faid=&g=&gsc=&h=&heid=&henid=&int=&m2rid=&meid=&ngid=522700&ngnid=2969153&side=&sort=","provenance":"Direkte kontrol af Rigsarkivets DAISY-arkivserieliste, 17.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-17"},{"title":"Primary archive series located for the 1811 probate/auction search","text":"DAISY lists a name register to the probate protocol (1794–1917), probate designations/death notifications (1795–1851), probate protocol for Sønder Djurs and Mols (1794–1824), auction protocol (1792–1814), a wider auction protocol (1753–1928), auction case files (1791–1928), and a justitsprotokol (1688–1919) with the 1803–1814 subseries. Rigsarkivet's Arkivalieronline lists the justitsprotokol as scanned and viewable online. The published G298 estate extract previously checked does not list Claus Gaardsted; this is a jurisdiction-level alternative, not evidence that a Claus file has been found. Search the probate name register and death notifications around 1811 first, then follow the entry into the 1794–1824 probate protocol; search the 1792–1814 auction protocol and auction case files separately for the widow's 1811 sale.","provenance":"Direkte kontrol af Rigsarkivet og publicerede skifteuddrag, 17.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-17"},{"title":"DAISY viser relevante skifte- og auktionsserier for 1811","text":"DAISY-oversigten for Djurs Sønder-Mols Herredsfoged viser en skiftedesignations-/dødsanmeldelsesserie 1795–1851, skifteprotokol for Sønder Djurs og Mols 1794–1824, auktionsprotokol 1792–1814 samt en bredere auktionsprotokol og auktionsdokumenter 1753–1928. Det giver en konkret primærkildeplan for Claus Gaardsteds død og Bogensholm-auktionen i 1811: søg først i dødsanmeldelser/skiftedesignationer efter Claus og enken, følg henvisningen til skifteprotokollen, og søg derefter auktionen i både den tidsafgrænsede og den bredere auktionsserie. DAISY-oversigten beviser ikke, at Claus' skifte findes i netop denne jurisdiktion; godsets egen skifteprotokol skal fortsat kontrolleres parallelt.","provenance":"Direkte kontrol af Rigsarkivets DAISY og arkivseriernes offentlige beskrivelser; kontrolleret 17.11.2026. De konkrete skifter, fæstebreve og auktioner er endnu ikke gennemset.","imported_on":"2026-11-17"},{"title":"Auktionsprotokoller kan være parallelle med auktionsdokumenter","text":"Arkivfortegnelsen skelner mellem auktionsprotokol 1792–1814, auktionsprotokol 1753–1928 og auktionsdokumenter 1791–1928. For auktionen over Bogensholm i 1811 bør alle tre serier søges: protokollen kan give kronologisk indførsel, mens auktionsdokumenterne kan indeholde bilag, konditioner, vurdering og købstilbud. Søg efter både Claus Gaardsted, enken og Bogensholm. En manglende post i den ene serie er ikke tilstrækkelig til at afvise, at sagen findes i de andre.","provenance":"Direkte kontrol af Rigsarkivets DAISY og arkivseriernes offentlige beskrivelser; kontrolleret 17.11.2026. De konkrete skifter, fæstebreve og auktioner er endnu ikke gennemset.","imported_on":"2026-11-17"}]},"evald-rousing-office":{"person_ids":["p76","p152"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[]},"liss-hoven-households":{"person_ids":["a88","a89","a90","a91"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"3. Hoven / Sønder Felding line","context":"3. Hoven / Sønder Felding line","text":"Hulda Christensen (`p11`) was born 25 September 1895 in Sønder Felding. Her father Mads Christensen/Kristensen (`p22`) was born 18 January 1852 in Hoven and married Karen Jensen in Grindsted in 1875. The Hoven birth entry identifies Mads's parents as Christen Andersen (`a44`) and Kirsten Christensdatter (`a45`).\n\nThe Hoven line has useful household evidence: Christen Andersen's mother Ane Kirstine Eskesdatter (`a89`) was a widow and owner of the Hoven mill in the 1834 census. Christen's household in 1860 included several children, including Mads. The mill ownership and widow household are useful collateral evidence for identity and chronology, but should not be treated as a substitute for the birth and marriage entries.","provenance":"Tidligere Liss-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Hoven mill","context":"10. Additional source targets recovered from the research extensions","text":"The Hoven line is tractable because the mill was a fixed property. The extension identifies:\n- Anders Jensen (`a88`), miller in Hoven, died before 1834;\n- Ane Kirstine Eskesdatter (`a89`), widow and owner of the Hoven mill in 1834;\n- Christen Pallesen (`a90`), farmer in Hoven, died before 1834;\n- Kirsten Marie Jensdatter (`a91`), farmer's widow in 1834 and aftægtskone 1840–1860.\n\nNext checks are Hoven baptisms 1770–1800 and marriages c.1770–1785, the Hoven mill deed and fire-insurance protocol, the 1834 matrikel, and a full reading of 1834 census household 124565. A mill deed/insurance record may establish property succession and occupation, but it does not replace the parish evidence for parentage.","provenance":"Tidligere Liss-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"gerda-lango-children":{"person_ids":["p62","p63"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"13. Langø household: two additional children to trace","context":"13. Langø household: two additional children to trace","text":"The family notes list seven children of Marius Michelsen Christensen (`p62`) and Ane Marie Jørgensen (`p63`). Two remain open:\n- **Christian Anton Christensen**, born 17 June 1879 in Skrøbelev, before his parents' marriage. Search Skrøbelev baptism, 1880/1890 censuses and the lægdsrulle to trace residence and later life.\n- **Christian Jørgen Christensen**, born 1 December 1887 in Copenhagen, confirmed in Humble in 1902; absent from Danish censuses after 1901 and possibly an emigrant. Search the 1901 and 1911 censuses and Copenhagen Police emigration protocols 1902–1920 by name and birth parish.\n\nThe extension says Valdemar Emil Christensen took over Langø and was father of Lisbeth Skov, author of “En Langø-piges erindringer”. The memoir is a family-held private print. Scan it and preserve provenance; consider depositing a copy with Langelands Museum or the local archive with the family's permission.","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-ove-identity":{"person_ids":["p216","p432","p433"],"sources":[{"label":"DBL, Hans Christopher Gedde","url":"https://biografiskleksikon.lex.dk/H.C._Gedde"},{"label":"Harbou, Slægten von Kleist i Danmark","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938476.pdf"},{"label":"Anker, Biografiske Efterretninger om norske Officerer","url":"https://arkivkopia.se/bok/runeberg-acj330bio"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Birth date","context":"5. Ove Samuel Gedde — birth date and maternal identity","text":"There is a real date conflict:\n- Harbou (1897) gives 28 February 1778–10 March 1843.\n- The current tree uses 28 February 1778.\n- P.I. Liebe / E. Borgstrøm's officer biography (based on Rigsarkiv military notes) gives 28 May 1778–10 March 1843.\n- Carl Johan Anker (1885) gives 28 May 1788; the OCR is marked unproofread and may conflate Ove with his father Hans Christopher.\n\nThe year 1778 has stronger support than 1788, but the exact day/month remains unresolved. Inspect the original baptism register in the relevant Fredensborg/Hof- og Slotsmenighed records and compare adjacent entries. Do not silently choose between 28 February and 28 May.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Fischer sisters and mother conflict","context":"5. Ove Samuel Gedde — birth date and maternal identity","text":"The 2025 expanded research export corrected the proposed parentage: it links Ove (1778) to Hans Christopher Gedde and his first wife **Frederikke Christiane Fischer** (baptised 4 November 1745 at Toten, died 28 February 1787), while **Øllegaard Sophie Fischer** (born 5 August 1752 at Ullensaker, died 9 December 1821) is treated as his stepmother, married to Hans Christopher on 15 December 1789. DBL's Hans Christopher entry distinguishes these as two sisters.\n\nHowever, the canonical `data/family.json` still links Ove to `p433`, Øllegaard Sophie Fischer, as mother. The expanded research export and canonical tree therefore disagree. This is a priority data-reconciliation issue, but do not change canonical parentage until the original baptism and marriage/death records have been checked.\n\nSources:\n- [DBL, Hans Christopher Gedde](https://biografiskleksikon.lex.dk/H.C._Gedde)\n- [Harbou, Slægten von Kleist i Danmark](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938476.pdf)\n- [Anker, Biografiske Efterretninger om norske Officerer](https://arkivkopia.se/bok/runeberg-acj330bio)","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Ove Samuel I / II hypothesis","context":"15. Gedde officer-line evidence conflicts and noble collateral ancestry","text":"The segment 12 extension argues for two men:\n- Ove Samuel I, born 28 February 1778, who died young;\n- Ove Samuel II, born 28 May 1788 in Fredrikstad, died 10 March 1843 in Helsingør and buried 16 March at Skt. Mariæ.\n\nIt argues that Harbou attached the 1778 birth to the man who married in 1806 and died in 1843. This is a plausible conflation hypothesis, but the 1778 infant's death and the 1788 Fredrikstad baptism have not been inspected. Keep one existing p216 record with the competing dates and maternal possibilities documented; do not create a second Ove or move the canonical FAMC until the Fredensborg/Fredrikstad baptism and burial records are read.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-gedde-christian":{"person_ids":["p864"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[]},"evald-vibygard-probate-1820":{"person_ids":["a66"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"Canonical ancestor: Hans Jensen (`a66`)","context":"3. Syv / Vibygård lead — keep two Hans Jensen households separate","text":"The current tree identifies Hans Jensen, former gardener at Vibygård, married Margrethe Pedersdatter in Syv in 1822. Their daughter Johanne Sophie Hansen was born in Syv in 1825. The 1834 census says Hans was a retired gardener at “Wiibygaard” and living on aftægt in Syv; he died in 1837. This explains why Johanne Sophie is sometimes recorded as born at Viby.","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Separate 1820 probate lead","context":"3. Syv / Vibygård lead — keep two Hans Jensen households separate","text":"A separate probate extract reportedly names **Maren Poulsdatter**, wife of Hans Jensen, gardener at Vibygård, and four adult children (Poul, Jens, Peder and Ane). This is not automatically the same Hans Jensen as `a66`, whose wife in the current tree is Margrethe Pedersdatter and whose marriage is in 1822. Keep the households separate until the original probate identifies the gardener, spouse, children and dates.","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"10. Additional Vibygaard probate detail — separate household pending identity","context":"10. Additional Vibygaard probate detail — separate household pending identity","text":"The 1820 Vibygaard Gods probate is cited as book 2, folio 21 (with related folios 22 and 32–34), dated 31 December 1820. The extension transcription identifies the deceased as **Maren Poulsdatter**, wife of a gardener Hans Jensen, and names four adult children:\n\n- Poul Hansen — smallholder in Assingløse;\n- Jens Hansen — gardener;\n- Peder Hansen — smallholder in Viby;\n- Ane Hansdatter — married to Willum Hansen, farmer in Valsø.\n\nThe same extension proposes that the husband was existing ancestor `a66`, Hans Jensen, former gardener at Vibygård, but the review correctly held that identity unresolved pending the original 1822 Syv marriage. The family structure within the 1820 probate may be sound while the identification with `a66` is not. Keep these four people and the 1820 widow in this dossier as an **unlinked Vibygaard household** until the 1820 original and 1822 Syv marriage have been compared by age, occupation, spouse, children and residence.\n\nArchive pointer: Vibygaard Gods skifteprotokol, book 2, film 41401, fol. 21, 22, 32–34. The prior online search did not locate the 1822 marriage; retry page-by-page in Arkivalieronline (Syv kirkebog, marriages 1822), then inspect the Vibygaard probate original and Hans Jensen's own probate if available.","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"liss-karen-jensen-date":{"person_ids":["p23"],"sources":[{"label":"Horsbøl — Sønder Omme 1870 census","url":"https://www.horsboel.dk/fkt/FKT_Sdr_Omme_1870.pdf"},{"label":"Horsbøl genealogy collection","url":"https://www.horsboel.dk/familie/grind/dat16.htm"},{"label":"Link-Lives Release 2 Guide (2025), section 2.5: exact person-appearance URL format","url":"https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/Link-lives-Guide-v.1.pdf"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Karen Jensen's birth-date discrepancy","context":"10. Additional source targets recovered from the research extensions","text":"Karen Jensen (`p23`) is recorded as born 29 August 1852 by the Skarrild baptism and the 1875 Grindsted marriage, while the 1901 census gives 29 July. Prefer the baptism/marriage evidence and preserve the 1901 census discrepancy in the event notes rather than silently normalising it.","provenance":"Tidligere Liss-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"gerda-nordenbro-probate-1787":{"person_ids":["p114"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"Family-held Sydlangeland death list","context":"15. South Langeland source preservation and the 1787 probate","text":"“Dødsliste for Sydlangeland 1900–1970” is a printed register held in the family's copy and cited repeatedly in the tree. It carries names, occupations, residences, ages and death dates, including entries otherwise difficult to find in indexed church books. Scan it at high resolution, preserve its title/creator/publication details and keep the original; the family notes suggest depositing a copy with Langelands Museum/local archive if the family agrees.","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"1787 Nordenbro probate — Christian Hansen","context":"15. South Langeland source preservation and the 1787 probate","text":"An open lead says a 1787 Nordenbro probate after **Christian Hansen** describes two marriages and several children in a local family. The extension suggests using it to test the identity of Christian Hansen (`p114`), born about 1806, who married Marie Madsdatter in 1829. The probate may concern a different Christian Hansen; the identity match is not established. Locate the correct probate jurisdiction and inspect the original for the deceased's age, spouse(s), children, residence and estate. Do not attach the family group to `p114` based only on the name and locality.","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Other open Langeland checks","context":"15. South Langeland source preservation and the 1787 probate","text":"- Verify the 1850 marriage of Jørgen Christensen (`p126`) and Bodil Margrethe Clausdatter (`p127`) in Skrøbelev/Magleby; their parents and nine children should be traced from the original records.\n- Inspect Fangel parish records for Anne Katrine Jensdatter (`p113`).\n- Inspect Snøde baptism records for Hans Berendsen (`p116`) and Anne Marie Olsdatter (`p117`).\n- Inspect Magleby/Humble records 1780–1800 for Claus Madsen (`a254`), Ane Mortensdatter (`a255`), Mads Hansen (`a508`) and Karen Clausdatter (`a509`).\n- Hennetved parish registers 1720–1800 are unindexed and are the route to the older Jørgensen branch (`a504`, `a505`, `a1008`–`a1011`).","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"16. Additional identity caution for the 1787 Nordenbro probate","context":"16. Additional identity caution for the 1787 Nordenbro probate","text":"The 1787 probate lead is explicitly described in the original extension as **not yet connected to the tree**. It concerns a deceased Christian Hansen in Nordenbro with two marriages and several children; one possible son of the right generation could be the Christian Hansen (`p114`) who married Marie Madsdatter in 1829. The 42-year gap is too long for the deceased Christian to be p114's father; the probate's children might be p114's generation or siblings. Read the probate for a son Christian born around 1806, the two wives' names and the guardian list. If no matching Christian appears, record the negative and close the lead. Source cited in the old extension: Magleby-Nordenbro gods probate series; a web lead was elgezira.dk/historie.html.","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"gerda-magleby-tved-collateral":{"person_ids":["g_nchr","g_imr","p120","p121"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[]},"evald-lovisa-mother-malmo":{"person_ids":["p17","p34"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"6. Jørgensen / Malmö line","context":"6. Jørgensen / Malmö line","text":"Lovisa (Louise) Larsson (`p17`) is already a canonical ancestor of Evald through Aage Evald Oskar Jørgensen. She was born in Malmö on 28 June 1862 and died in Slagelse on 12 October 1910. Her death entry names Niels Larsen (`p34`) as her Swedish father; no mother is recorded in the current tree. The original potential-lead search for Lovisa should therefore be treated as partly resolved, not as an unconnected new person. Further work should inspect Malmö birth and household records to identify her mother and confirm the birth entry.\n\nNiels Jørgensen (`p32`) married Johanne Sophie Hansdatter (`p33`) in Vor Frue, Copenhagen, on 7 July 1854. His birthplace is transcribed as “Hove/Høje sogn, Svendborg amt”; the parish is not identified. Preserve both readings until the original marriage entry is re-examined.","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-krogh-family":{"person_ids":["p12","p6"],"sources":[{"label":"Lokalhistorisk Arkiv Ulkebøl – Wilhelm Krogh (sekundær slægtsregistrering)","url":"https://www.ulkeboelarkiv.dk/slaegt/index.php?route=/slaegt/tree/tree2/individual/I80148/Vilhelm-Krogh"},{"label":"Lokalhistorisk Arkiv Ulkebøl – Bothille Marie Hansen (sekundær kontrol)","url":"https://www.ulkeboelarkiv.dk/slaegt/index.php?route=/slaegt/tree/tree2/individual/I80800/Bothille-Marie-Hansen"},{"label":"Lokalhistorisk Arkiv Ulkebøl – Peter Krogh (sekundær kontrol)","url":"https://www.ulkeboelarkiv.dk/slaegt/index.php?route=/slaegt/tree/tree2/individual/I30804/Peter-Krogh"},{"label":"Lokalhistorisk Arkiv Ulkebøl – arkivets kontakt og samlingsbeskrivelse","url":"https://www.ulkeboelarkiv.dk/om-arkivet/"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Wilhelm Krogh's origins and family","context":"12. Additional recovered leads: German-period Sønderjylland, Nybøl and Gedde collateral","text":"The extension gives a reported birth for Wilhelm Krogh (`p12`) of 9 October 1908 at a place read as either **Tastrup** or **Vastrup**, Kreis Flensburg. The place name must remain ambiguous until the 11 July 1932 Egernsund marginal note is reread at high magnification. Search the 1908 birth register at Kreisarchiv Schleswig-Flensburg, after identifying the correct Standesamt jurisdiction.\n\nA possible brother is Detlef Krogh, born 8 May 1905, who reportedly married in Broager in 1929 and had daughters registered in Nybøl in 1936 and 1940. The extension also notes a Detlef Krogh (born 1849) and Botilde (born 1850) in the 1921 Nybøl census as possible older relatives. These are **family-identification leads**, not established sibling or grandparent links. The best next step is the 1905 Detlef's 1929 Broager marriage entry, which should name his father; compare it with Wilhelm's 1908 German birth entry.\n\nFor Sønderjylland after 1867, use German Standesamt civil registers and Kreisarchiv holdings as well as the relevant church books. The old extension identifies Broagerlands Lokalarkiv's teglværk personnel protocols as another possible source for Egernsund workers.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Older Lorenz namesake warning","context":"12. Additional recovered leads: German-period Sønderjylland, Nybøl and Gedde collateral","text":"The research extension also distinguishes an earlier Lorenz Heinrich Frederiksen (`p24`) in the Broager line from another namesake (`pf12`) in Egernsund, and from this grandparent (`p6`, born 1932). Keep these people separate by birth date, parents, spouse and parish. Do not merge on the shared forenames.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Wilhelm Krogh: separate 1908 namesake candidate","context":"16. Broager / Nybøl / Rinkenæs collateral records and name traps","text":"The evidence audit notes a possible **Carl Vilhelm Krogh**, reportedly born 9 November 1908 in Tastrup/Flensburg and recorded in a 1925 Tandslet census, while this project's Wilhelm Krogh (`p12`) is recorded as born 9 October 1908 in Tastrup or Vastrup. Different given names and a one-month date difference mean they must not be merged. Request the 1908 Adelby church-book volume (book 2.3) from the Kirchenbuchamt Schleswig-Flensburg and compare exact parents and date with the Egernsund marginal note. The earlier candidate Detlef Krogh (born 1905) and the reported 1929 Broager marriage remain another route to the family.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-pakjaer-history":{"person_ids":["p6","sofus","sofus_ff"],"sources":[{"label":"Fra Nybøl sogn nr. 3 – lokalhistorisk hæfte (biblioteksscanning)","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2025/953243.pdf"},{"label":"Nybøl 1921-folketælling – bevaret kildescanning","url":"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sloev/tree/main/images/nybol-folketaelling-1921.jpg"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Paakjær farm records and the Sophus line","context":"12. Additional recovered leads: German-period Sønderjylland, Nybøl and Gedde collateral","text":"Mathias Petersen (`sofus_ff`) reportedly built Paakjær in Nybøl in 1879; the farm was later called “Sofus minde”. This gives a collateral archival route: building application, fire-insurance policy and property records may identify the builder, owners and estate history. These records can corroborate household/property context but do not prove Lorenz's paternity.\n\nThe Nybøl 1921 census scan and Sophus's 1903 baptism/grave scan are preserved in `leads/` and linked above. For the possible half-sibling group, the current citations cover Nybøl girls born 1938, 1940 and 1943. Read the register beyond 1943 before assuming a fourth daughter; if the Norway lead is correct, search Norwegian marriage banns and newspaper notices after identifying her full name.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-gedde-emigration":{"person_ids":["p6"],"sources":[{"label":"Rigsarkivet: Udvandring til andre verdensdele – vejledning","url":"https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/udvandring-til-andre-verdensdele-kom-godt-i-gang/"},{"label":"Arkivalieronline: Københavns Politi, udvandringsarkiver","url":"https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/other/other-collection/168"},{"label":"DAISY: Københavns Politi – udvandringsserier","url":"https://daisy.rigsarkivet.dk/arkivskaber_og_arkivserie_liste?a=k%C3%B8benhavns+politi&b=udvandr&d=1&e=2022"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"8. Other Gedde and emigration leads","context":"8. Other Gedde and emigration leads","text":"- **Ove Gjedde / Brostrup Gjedde:** the older admiral Ove Gjedde (1594–1660) and Brostrup Gjedde are not automatically the same family as the later Danish/Norwegian officer-family Gedde. The Store norske leksikon notes the two may not be connected. Keep separate unless Schøller and primary records establish a link.\n- **Olaf Gedde, Shanghai, 1904:** search passport, shipping, consular and commercial records; destination may be intermediate.\n- **Emil Gedde, Argentina, 1922:** search emigration and Argentine civil/immigration records; preserve Gedde/Giedde spelling.\n- **Wilhelm Krogh, reported birth 9 October 1908, “Tastrup/Vastrup”, Kreis Flensburg:** locality reading is uncertain. Preserve both candidate spellings until the historical place and Standesamt jurisdiction are established.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Emigration leads with specific contract details","context":"12. Additional recovered leads: German-period Sønderjylland, Nybøl and Gedde collateral","text":"- **Olaf Gedde → Shanghai:** Copenhagen Police emigration contract no. 3534, dated 11 July 1904 (agent Haugsted), is already transcribed in the extension. Follow the contract through shipping and Danish consular records in Shanghai.\n- **Emil Gedde → Argentina:** two photographs from the Eldorado colony are cited as B9886 and B9790. Search the colony register at Museo Eldorado and Danish-Argentine congregation books, in addition to emigration and passenger records.\n- **Ove Gjedde vs Gedde:** the old admiral Ove Gjedde/Tranquebar line and the later bourgeois officer-family Gedde must remain separate. The shared surname is not proof of kinship.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-hammer-fosterage":{"person_ids":["p27","p54","p108"],"sources":[{"label":"Familiefotografi fra 1895 – bevaret i projektets kildemateriale","url":"media/familien-gedde-1895.jpg"},{"label":"Rigsarkivet: Kirkebøger – kom godt i gang","url":"https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboeger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/"},{"label":"Familien Gedde, 1895","url":"media/familien-gedde-1895.jpg"},{"label":"Dybbøl register index","url":"https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/other/index-creator/29/725983/18484622"},{"label":"Dybbøl Ab 4","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2024/941454.pdf"},{"label":"Rigsarkivet's church-book guide","url":"https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboeger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Herlufmagle fosterage and the Hammer name","context":"12. Additional recovered leads: German-period Sønderjylland, Nybøl and Gedde collateral","text":"Paula Mathilde Christle Gedde (`p27`) is reported to have grown up in Herlufmagle parsonage with her aunt Christine Sophie von Kleist and the pastor Dr. theol. Edvard Snedorph Hammer. Edvard Hammer is Paula's foster/social father figure; the middle name of Paula's son Edvard Hammer Gedde (`p54`) is explained as honouring him. Preserve this as a foster/household relationship, not biological parentage. The family photograph from 1895 is an important captioned source for Paula's place among Edvard Hammer Gedde and Vitta Ludvigsen's children.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"evald-claus-probate":{"person_ids":["p72"],"sources":[{"label":"Rolsøgård G302 index, 1778–1816","url":"https://slaegt.dk/media/jnnnvuml/alfabetisk-navneliste-og-skifteuddrag-til-rols%C3%B8g%C3%A5rd-gods-g302-mols-herred-1778-1816-1818.pdf"},{"label":"Rigsarkivet DAISY: Djurs Sønder-Mols Herredsfoged, probate, auction and court series","url":"https://daisy.rigsarkivet.dk/arkivskabers_arkivserier?a=&b=&c=Djurs+S%C3%B8nde+herred&d=1&dir=&e=2022&ed=&ep=&epid=&es=&f=&faid=&g=&gsc=&h=&heid=&henid=&int=&m2rid=&meid=&ngid=522700&ngnid=2969153&side=&sort="},{"label":"Arkivalieronline: Djurs Sønder-Mols Herredsfoged, Justitsprotokol 1688–1919 (scanned)","url":"https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/other-collection/35"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Claus Gaardsted (`p72`) and Karen Christensdatter","context":"2. Gaardsted / Vosnæsgaard / Rolsøgård","text":"**Status: strong sibling clue; parentage unresolved.**\n\nThe Rolsøgård G302 alphabetical probate index, under Niels Jensen Degn in Vrinners, probate no. 11, 14 May 1787, folio 39, records:\n\n> “Arv i boet til afdødes stedbørn efter Christen Christensen Tohde? foged i Dejret (skifte 1764) til datter Karen Christensdatter LV: broder Claus Gaardsted forpagter på Vosnæsgård.”\n\nThe index identifies Karen Christensdatter as sister of Claus Gaardsted, tenant at Vosnæsgaard, and says he acted as her lavværge (legal guardian). It points to an earlier 1764 probate for bailiff Christen Christensen “Tohde?” in Dejret.\n\n**Do not infer** that Tohde was Claus's or Karen's biological father. The wording refers to stepchildren and is not sufficiently clear to establish biological parentage. The original 1764 probate must be located and read. Also inspect the original 1787 folio 39 rather than relying solely on the alphabetical index.\n\nSource: [Rolsøgård G302 index, 1778–1816](https://slaegt.dk/media/jnnnvuml/alfabetisk-navneliste-og-skifteuddrag-til-rols%C3%B8g%C3%A5rd-gods-g302-mols-herred-1778-1816-1818.pdf).","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Primary archive series located for the 1811 probate/auction search","text":"DAISY lists a name register to the probate protocol (1794–1917), probate designations/death notifications (1795–1851), probate protocol for Sønder Djurs and Mols (1794–1824), auction protocol (1792–1814), a wider auction protocol (1753–1928), auction case files (1791–1928), and a justitsprotokol (1688–1919) with the 1803–1814 subseries. Rigsarkivet's Arkivalieronline lists the justitsprotokol as scanned and viewable online. The published G298 estate extract previously checked does not list Claus Gaardsted; this is a jurisdiction-level alternative, not evidence that a Claus file has been found. Search the probate name register and death notifications around 1811 first, then follow the entry into the 1794–1824 probate protocol; search the 1792–1814 auction protocol and auction case files separately for the widow's 1811 sale.","provenance":"Direkte kontrol af Rigsarkivet og publicerede skifteuddrag, 17.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-17"}]},"evald-jochum-baptism":{"person_ids":["p36"],"sources":[{"label":"Jochum's baptism scan","url":"media/jochum-baptism-skodstrup-1792.jpg"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Jochum (Joachim) Gaardsted (`p36`), born 1792","context":"2. Gaardsted / Vosnæsgaard / Rolsøgård","text":"The Skødstrup church-book entry, 1792, p.75 / BSID 736476 image 43, records the baptism of Jochum on 2 October and names “Hr. Forpagter Gaardsted paa Vosnæsgaard” and wife Kirstine Marie Jørgensdatter. The family tree identifies the parents as Claus Gaardsted and Kirsten Marie Jørgensdatter. The record supports the Vosnæsgaard household and the father's status/name; inspect the complete original page for the exact date and wording.\n\nImage: [Jochum's baptism scan](../images/jochum-baptism-skodstrup-1792.jpg).","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"evald-jorgensen-collateral":{"person_ids":["p32","p33"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"15. Jørgensen siblings and descendants — collateral to the direct line","context":"15. Jørgensen siblings and descendants — collateral to the direct line","text":"The older Jørgensen frontier notes identify two children of Niels Jørgensen (`p32`) and Johanne Sophie Hansen (`p33`) who survived infancy:\n- **Hans Sophus Frederik Jørgensen** (`s_p32_0`), possementmager;\n- **Peter Jørgensen** (`s_p32_2`), shoemaker.\n\nTheir marriages and descendants remain open. The four other children who died in infancy are already dated and cited in the source tree. For the two surviving sons, inspect Copenhagen marriage records, artisan guild/trade registers (håndværkerprotokoller), address registers and civil property records. This is collateral research, not a missing direct-ancestor link.","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"liss-foster-households":{"person_ids":["p10","p11","p22","p23"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"5. Foster / household relationships","context":"5. Foster / household relationships","text":"The worklist notes foster/plejebørn in local census records in the Pårup Melgaard / heath-parish branch. Record these as foster or household relationships, not biological children. For each child, capture the exact census year, household position, wording and named parents if present. Do not convert a census household into a parent-child link without corroboration.","provenance":"Tidligere Liss-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-sophus-daughters":{"person_ids":["p6","sofus"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"3. Sophus Petersen's reported daughters / possible half-siblings","context":"3. Sophus Petersen's reported daughters / possible half-siblings","text":"The family account says Sophus and wife Christine had four daughters and that one moved to Norway. The current tree note elsewhere names only three daughters (Gerda and two others), while the family document says “4 døtre”. This is a count discrepancy. Nybøl church-book searches are reported for girls born 1938, 1940 and 1943; a fourth daughter has not been located. Recheck the full family group and a wider date range. Do not assume a fourth daughter solely from the count without recording the source for it.\n\nIf the relationship to Sophus is supported, the daughters would be possible maternal/paternal half-siblings depending on the exact parentage, but this is downstream of the paternity question and should not be used as proof of it.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-dybbol-marriage":{"person_ids":["p52","p53","p26"],"sources":[{"label":"Rigsarkivet: Dybbøl vielsesregister 1723–1900","url":"https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/other/index-creator/29/725983/18484622"},{"label":"Arkivoversigt: Dybbøl Ab 4, vielser 1861–1906","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2024/941454.pdf"},{"label":"Rigsarkivet's church-book guide","url":"https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboeger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"9. Dybbøl / Broager collateral line","context":"9. Dybbøl / Broager collateral line","text":"Jens Jensen (`p26`), father of Else Gedde Jensen, is recorded as son of Rasmus Jensen and Christine Hansen. He married Paula Gedde in 1907. Rasmus Jensen (`p52`) married Anne Christine Hansen (`p53`) in Dybbøl on 18 September 1866. The exact marriage entry remains to be visually inspected; prior Arkivalieronline access returned a viewer shell. The archival series description confirms that Dybbøl Ab 4 (1861–1927) contains marriages 1861–1906; a separate official Rigsarkivet index for Dybbøl marriages 1723–1900 is also catalogued, but its image viewer currently exposes only a shell to this research pass. These catalogue descriptions confirm where to look, not the contents of the 1866 entry. Transcribe witnesses, ages, residences and fathers from the original page before treating the proposed parents Hans Hansen and Ellen Kock as independently verified.\n\n**Catalogue confirmation (16 November 2026):** Rigsarkivet's [Dybbøl register index](https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/other/index-creator/29/725983/18484622) lists a marriage index for 1723–1900. The printed archival inventory confirms that [Dybbøl Ab 4](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2024/941454.pdf) covers marriages 1861–1906. Neither catalogue/index description has yet yielded a readable transcription of the 18 September 1866 entry. See also [Rigsarkivet's church-book guide](https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/).","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-kleist-medieval-audit":{"person_ids":["p868","p3472"],"sources":[{"label":"Von Kleist Familienverband: 2025 slægtshistorie","url":"https://www.v-kleist.com/FG/Dubberow.pdf"},{"label":"H. W. Harbou: Slægten von Kleist i Danmark (1897)","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938476.pdf"},{"label":"H. P. Selmer, *Nekrologiske Samlinger*, 1848–49, s. 68 og 114 (PDF)","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938488.pdf"},{"label":"Brejl, private personarkiver, post om gravbrev for Frederik Christian von Kleist","url":"https://brejl.dk/privatB.html"},{"label":"Muttrin 2025","url":"https://www.v-kleist.com/FG/Muttrin/Muttrin.pdf"},{"label":"Allgemeine Geschichte","url":"https://www.v-kleist.com/FG/Genealogie/AllgemeineGeschichte.pdf"},{"label":"Harbou 1897","url":"https://www.v-kleist.com/FG_allg/Kleist_in_Daenemark.pdf"},{"label":"English Tychow-Dubberow translation, p.123","url":"https://www.v-kleist.com/FG/english/Dubberow_translation.pdf"},{"label":"H.
