The supplied material lacks precise page/column references or transcriptions sufficient to add these parent-child links.\n\n**Next check:** inspect the cited Zedler article, *Grosses vollständiges Universal-Lexicon*, vol. 59, “Woyden, Woyten”, and identify the exact statements and their underlying sources.","provenance":"Tværgående forskningsledger, indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"3. von Stojentin maternal ancestry — possible, not established","context":"3. von Stojentin maternal ancestry — possible, not established","text":"The dossier suggests Hans (Johann) von Stojentin (1446–1517), lord of Gohren and Landvogt of Stolp, may have been Elisabeth von Stojentin of Giesebitz's father. It further names Peter von Stojentin (c. 1400–1470) and Elisabeth von Lettow as Hans's parents.\n\nThe dossier itself describes the connection to Elisabeth of Giesebitz as probable rather than demonstrated. An estate association with Giesebitz is not proof of parentage. No link should be added until the identity and relationship are corroborated.","provenance":"Tværgående forskningsledger, indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"6.4 Christina von Woyten — possible collateral children","context":"6. Branch expansion leads from the expanded master dossier","text":"The expanded dossier describes Hedwig Maria and “collateral siblings” as Christina's children. This conflicts with the von Kleist family archive translation, p. 123, which identifies Hedwig Maria as Georg and Christina's only legitimate child. The word “legitimate” leaves a research question about any other biological children, but does not prove that such children existed.\n\nSearch the cited Zedler article and contemporary church, probate or estate records for individually named children and explicit parentage. Add any well-supported non-marital children without excluding them because of status, but do not manufacture siblings from a general phrase.","provenance":"Tværgående forskningsledger, indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-danish-kleist-1775":{"person_ids":["p868"],"sources":[{"label":"Selmer: Nekrologiske Samlinger 1848–49","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938488.pdf"},{"label":"H. W. Harbou: Slægten von Kleist i Danmark (1897)","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938476.pdf"},{"label":"Kolding kirkebøger: ældre arkivoversigt","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/807141.pdf"},{"label":"Brejl, private personarkiver, post om gravbrev for Frederik Christian von Kleist","url":"https://brejl.dk/privatB.html"},{"label":"Kolding Stadsarkiv: Slægtsforskning og Ingrid Bruuns afskrifter","url":"https://koldingstadsarkiv.dk/brug-arkivet/slaegtsforskning"},{"label":"Rigsarkivet: Kirkebøger – kom godt i gang","url":"https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/"},{"label":"Rigsarkivet, Arkivalieronline: Kolding Sankt Nikolaj navne-register 1635–1891","url":"https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/other/index-creator/29/534415/17148233"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Follow-up: resolving the 1743–1848 Danish branch (16 November 2026)","context":"Follow-up: resolving the 1743–1848 Danish branch (16 November 2026)","text":"A closer reading of Harbou's complete entry (not just the Christian Adam paragraph) sharpens the earlier correction and identifies one likely source of the supplied 1774–1848 name/date combination.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Christian Adam III.466's actual descendants in Harbou","context":"Follow-up: resolving the 1743–1848 Danish branch (16 November 2026)","text":"Harbou gives Christian Adam III.466 and Sophie Rosenkrantz five children. Their eldest surviving son was **Christian Frederik III.601**, born 25 August 1743, died at Bredsted 12 July 1799; he married Anna Margrethe Schubart on 3 December 1770. Harbou lists four sons and four daughters from that marriage:\n\n- Christine Sophie, born 28 August 1771, died 31 March 1862; married Edvard Snedorph Hammer in 1795.\n- Adam Ludvig Vilhelm (III.725), born 16 October 1772, died 6 October 1851 at Viborggaard.\n- Valentin Ulrik (III.726), born 31 December 1773, died 23 July 1827; married Anna Matthea Bierg on 30 November 1811. Their children include Margrethe Christine Elisabeth (1813), a daughter who died in infancy (1814), Frederik Christian III.853 (1815–1892), Holgerine Henriette Caroline (1817–1845), Christian Conrad Sophus (1820–1880), Aadamine Frederikke Christiane (1822–1874), Riise Sophie Emilie (1824–1864), and Johan Eduard Valentin (1825).\n- **Frederik Christian (III.727), born 8 January 1775, died 21 May 1848.** This is a plausible identification behind the supplied “Christian Frederik Vilhelm” (1774–1848), but Harbou's name and birth date differ. It is a candidate identity, not a proven match; compare the supplied source and primary records before merging.\n- Carl Gottlieb (III.728), born 15 January 1778, died 26 July 1849.\n- Juliane Frederikke Margrethe, born 2 December 1781, died 22 May 1861; married Norwegian major-general Nicolai Wilhelm Gedde in 1811.\n- Frederikke Louise Dorthea, born 5 January 1786, died 24 January 1870; married Ove Samuel Gedde in 1806.\n- Henriette Louise, born 30 November 1795, died 12 April 1827; married Johan Adolf von der Recke in 1813.\n\nHarbou also lists Emil Christian (III.601a), baptized 25 June 1745 and died 13 September 1745; Carl Christian (III.601b), baptized 9 June 1749 and died 15 August 1750; and an unnamed daughter who died young in 1753. The 1743–1799 Christian Frederik is therefore not to be confused with his younger brother(s) or with later descendants.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"What this changes about the supplied pedigree","context":"Follow-up: resolving the 1743–1848 Danish branch (16 November 2026)","text":"The 1774–1848 entry may be a distorted rendering of Harbou's **Frederik Christian III.727 (1775–1848)**, a son of Christian Frederik III.601, rather than a distinct “Christian Frederik Vilhelm.” If the supplied source's individual has the same spouse, children, military/civil career or place of death, that would help establish the match. At present, the identity remains a hypothesis because the forename and birth year do not agree.\n\nThe claimed **Sophie Frederikke (1776–1851)** is not identified in Harbou's list of Christian Frederik III.601's children. It could be a different individual, a spouse, or a conflation; no relationship is added. Likewise, the 1742/43–1815 “Frederik Carl Emil” is not one of the children Harbou lists for Christian Adam III.466. It remains unplaced.\n\nThe earlier Copenhagen branch correction stands: the 1813–1825 people named above are children of Valentin Ulrik III.726 and Anna Matthea Bierg, not of the supplied 1770s couple. Harbou's source gives Margrethe's birth as 25 August 1813, Frederik Christian III.853's birth as 8 October 1815, and Holgerine's marriage to Hans Lindholm as 13 October 1838.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Research priority","context":"Follow-up: resolving the 1743–1848 Danish branch (16 November 2026)","text":"Compare the original pedigree's entry for “Christian Frederik Vilhelm” against Harbou's III.727 and the 1775 baptism, military records and 1848 death/burial record. Then trace III.727's marriage and descendants from Danish parish registers and probate. Separately identify the source and parents for “Frederik Carl Emil” and “Sophie Frederikke”; do not force either into the Christian Adam line.\n\n**Assessment:** Harbou now provides a detailed, internally coherent branch from Christian Adam III.466 through Christian Frederik III.601 and his eight children. One supplied 1774–1848 figure may correspond to Frederik Christian III.727, but the name/date discrepancy must be resolved. No canonical tree records or links are changed.\n\nSource: [H. W. Harbou, “Slægten von Kleist i Danmark” (1897), especially the full entry for Christian Adam III.466 and children of Christian Frederik III.601](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938476.pdf), searchable text at pp. 196–242 of the transcription.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Follow-up: locating the 1848 death evidence for Frederik Christian III.727 (16 November 2026)","context":"Follow-up: locating the 1848 death evidence for Frederik Christian III.727 (16 November 2026)","text":"This check follows the unresolved identity question between Harbou's Frederik Christian III.727 (born 8 January 1775; died 21 May 1848) and the supplied “Christian Frederik Vilhelm” (1774–1848). No identity merge or canonical data change is justified.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"What the cited source actually says","context":"Follow-up: locating the 1848 death evidence for Frederik Christian III.727 (16 November 2026)","text":"H. W. Harbou, “Slægten von Kleist i Danmark,” *Personalhistorisk Tidsskrift* (1897), in the entry for Christian Adam III.466 and his children, gives III.727's name, birth date, and military progression (page, cadet, officer in the Sjællandske Jægerkorps, company commander, major, and finally brevet/character lieutenant-colonel), ending only with “† 21/5 1848.” The entry does **not** name a death place or burial place. Harbou's footnote 36 points to H. P. Selmer, *Nekrologiske Samlinger*, vol. II. This is the immediate next secondary source to inspect for a fuller death notice and possible residence/place of death. The earlier suggestion that he died at Bredsted is not established by this passage and should not be repeated as fact.\n\nHarbou separately states that III.601 (Christian Frederik, born 1743) owned property near Bredsted and died there in 1799. That Bredsted location belongs to III.601, not III.727; avoid transferring it to his younger brother.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Archive catalogue route identified","context":"Follow-up: locating the 1848 death evidence for Frederik Christian III.727 (16 November 2026)","text":"The Landeskirchliches Archiv der Evangelisch-Lutherischen Kirche in Norddeutschland's Archion catalogue lists the following digitised church-book series:\n- Bredstedt, *Bestattungen 1831–1879*, covering the year 1848; catalogue parish page also lists *Namensverzeichnis (Taufen, Trauungen, Bestattungen) 1714–1900*, local signature Bredstedt_13.\n- Schleswig-Domgemeinde, *Bestattungen 1823–1856*, also covering 1848; the parish catalogue additionally lists a burial-name index for 1770–1874.\n\nThe catalogue pages confirm that these volumes exist and are held by the Landeskirchliches Archiv; they do not establish that III.727 appears in either register. The Archion catalogue indicates account-gated access to the digitised images. No original register image has yet been inspected. Because Harbou does not supply a place of death, both parish routes remain conditional rather than proof that either was his parish.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Source and next action","context":"Follow-up: locating the 1848 death evidence for Frederik Christian III.727 (16 November 2026)","text":"- Harbou, *Slægten von Kleist i Danmark* (1897), entry III.727 and footnote 36: https://www.v-kleist.com/FG_allg/Kleist_in_Daenemark.pdf (transcription lines 229–230 and 252 in the online text).\n- Archion, Bredstedt parish catalogue and index: https://www.archion.de/de/alle-archive/schleswig-holstein/landeskirchliches-archiv-der-evangelisch-lutherischen-kirche-in-norddeutschland/kirchenkreis-nordfriesland/bredstedt/266084\n- Archion, Schleswig-Domgemeinde catalogue: https://www.archion.de/de/alle-archive/schleswig-holstein/landeskirchliches-archiv-der-evangelisch-lutherischen-kirche-in-norddeutschland/kirchenkreis-schleswig-flensburg/schleswig-domgemeinde\n\nNext: inspect Selmer vol. II at the precise reference Harbou intended, first to identify the death locality and any obituary details. Then search the appropriate 1848 burial register; if Selmer gives no locality, use the Schleswig and Bredstedt name indexes as a bounded two-parish search, while checking the officer's last known residence/service record. Compare any result against age, rank, family and date before identifying it as III.727. Keep the “Christian Frederik Vilhelm” match speculative until independent identity attributes agree.\n\n**Assessment:** Harbou securely records III.727's 1775 birth, career and 21 May 1848 death as a published genealogical claim, but does not provide a locality in this entry. The archive catalogues identify two possible register series, not a confirmed burial. No new person, date, relationship or canonical edge is added.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Follow-up: Selmers nekrolog og begravelsen for Frederik Christian af Kleist (16. november 2026)","context":"Follow-up: Selmers nekrolog og begravelsen for Frederik Christian af Kleist (16. november 2026)","text":"Den samtidige *Nekrologiske Samlinger* 1848–49 er nu fundet digitaliseret, og den indeholder en biografi, der matcher Harbous Frederik Christian III.727.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Samtidig nekrolog — biografisk identifikation","context":"Follow-up: Selmers nekrolog og begravelsen for Frederik Christian af Kleist (16. november 2026)","text":"H. P. Selmer, *Nekrologiske Samlinger*, 1848–49, s. 68, omtaler **“Oberstløjtnant Frederik Christian af Kleist”**. Han beskrives som:\n- født **3. januar 1775 i Kolding**;\n- søn af kammerherre Christian Frederik af Kleist og Anne Margrethe født Schubarth;\n- først page hos Christian VII, derefter landkadet og omkring 1792/1793 fændrik ved Sjællandske Jægerkorps;\n- senere kaptajn og kompagnichef;\n- afskediget 1. marts 1816 på grund af svagelighed med pension som major, senere med oberstløjtnants afsked;\n- død **21. maj 1848**.\n\nSelmers dødsfortegnelse i samme bind, s. 114, anfører igen oberstløjtnant Frederik Christian af Kleist, død efter mange lidelser, 73 år gammel. Kombinationen af navn, rang, dødsdato, alder, forældre og militær karriere gør identifikationen med Harbous III.727 meget stærk. Den bekræfter dog ikke den alternative identifikation med den fremlagte “Christian Frederik Vilhelm” (1774–1848).","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Vigtig fødselsdatoafvigelse","context":"Follow-up: Selmers nekrolog og begravelsen for Frederik Christian af Kleist (16. november 2026)","text":"Harbou angiver **8. januar 1775**, mens Selmer angiver **3. januar 1775** og fødestedet Kolding. Bevar begge datoer som kildevarianter, indtil Koldings dåbsindførsel er kontrolleret. Selmers forældreoplysninger passer til Harbous Christian Frederik III.601 og Anna Margrethe Schubart.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Begravelse","context":"Follow-up: Selmers nekrolog og begravelsen for Frederik Christian af Kleist (16. november 2026)","text":"Brejls oversigt over private personarkiver omtaler et 100-årigt gravbrev dateret 27. maj 1848 for et gravsted på Garnisons Kirkegård uden Østerport. Heri er oberstløjtnant Frederik Christian von Kleist registreret som nedsat **26. maj 1848**. Det giver en konkret begravelsesdato og kirkegård, men oversigten er en sekundær registrering af et gravbrev — ikke selve gravbrevet eller kirkegårdens originale begravelsesprotokol. Den dokumenterer derfor begravelsesstedet bedre end dødsstedet; den beviser ikke, at han døde i København.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Bibliografisk note","context":"Follow-up: Selmers nekrolog og begravelsen for Frederik Christian af Kleist (16. november 2026)","text":"Harbous note 36 henviser til “Selmer, Nekrolog. Saml. II”. Den matchende biografi og dødsfortegnelse er fundet i det digitaliserede bind **1848–49**, som er bind I i værkets tobindsudgave. Henvisningens bindnummer bør kontrolleres mod Harbous originale tryk/udgave, men det ændrer ikke indholdet af den fundne samtidige nekrolog.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Kilder","context":"Follow-up: Selmers nekrolog og begravelsen for Frederik Christian af Kleist (16. november 2026)","text":"- [H. P. Selmer, *Nekrologiske Samlinger*, 1848–49, s. 68 og 114 (PDF)](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938488.pdf).\n- [Harbou, *Slægten von Kleist i Danmark* (1897), Frederik Christian III.727 og note 36](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2023/938476.pdf).\n- [Brejl, private personarkiver, post om gravbrev for Frederik Christian von Kleist](https://brejl.dk/privatB.html).\n\n**Vurdering:** Frederik Christian af Kleist i Selmers nekrolog er med stor sandsynlighed Harbous III.727. Dødsdatoen 21. maj 1848 bekræftes uafhængigt af Harbou; Selmer giver fødselsdatoen 3. januar, mens Harbou giver 8. januar. Begravelse på Garnisons Kirkegård 26. maj er registreret i Brejls oversigt, men det originale gravbrev/protokol og selve dødsstedet mangler stadig. Ingen kanoniske personer eller slægtskabsrelationer ændres.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Follow-up: Kolding Stadsarkivs afskrifter som vej til dåben (16. november 2026)","context":"Follow-up: Kolding Stadsarkivs afskrifter som vej til dåben (16. november 2026)","text":"En konkret, lokal kildevej er identificeret til at afgøre fødselsdatokonflikten mellem Selmers 3. januar og Harbous 8. januar 1775.\n\nKolding Stadsarkiv oplyser, at lokalhistorikeren Ingrid Bruuns arkiv indeholder afskrifter af kirkebøger fra Kolding Kommune for perioden 1635–1881. Afskrifterne er digitaliseret og frit tilgængelige via en søgeportal. Det er et nyttigt søgehjælpemiddel til at lokalisere den relevante indførsel og kontrollere navnevarianter, men afskriften bør sammenholdes med den originale kirkebog, før dato og forældre anses for endeligt fastslået.\n\nKolding Stadsarkivs side om slægtsforskning linker direkte til Ingrid Bruuns afskrifter:\nhttps://koldingstadsarkiv.dk/brug-arkivet/slaegtsforskning\n\n**Status:** Søgeportalen (salldata.dk/ibp/index.php) gav timeout under denne undersøgelse. Der er derfor endnu ikke fundet eller læst en konkret dåbsindførsel for Frederik Christian. Dette er en ny søgevej, ikke ny persondokumentation.\n\nNæste trin er at søge i Kolding-afskrifterne på Frederik/Frederich Christian von Kleist og varianter af forældrenes navne, afgrænset til januar 1775. Derefter skal originalbilledet i Arkivalieronline kontrolleres. Rigsarkivets vejledning beskriver, at kirkebøger før ca. 1814 normalt findes som enesteministerialbøger, ført kronologisk; Kolding Stadsarkivs afskrifter kan hjælpe med at finde stedet i den originale bog.\n\nKilder:\n- [Kolding Stadsarkiv: Slægtsforskning og Ingrid Bruuns afskrifter](https://koldingstadsarkiv.dk/brug-arkivet/slaegtsforskning).\n- [Rigsarkivet: Kirkebøger – kom godt i gang](https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/).\n\n**Vurdering:** Der er nu en konkret og relevant søgeportal for Kolding 1775, men fødselsdatoen er fortsat uafklaret, indtil afskrift og originalindførsel er fundet og sammenholdt.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Follow-up: independent catalogue evidence for Kolding's 1775 baptism search (16 November 2026)","context":"Follow-up: independent catalogue evidence for Kolding's 1775 baptism search (16 November 2026)","text":"A further check of Kolding's historical church-book holdings identifies a more specific archival route, but still does not reveal the baptism entry itself.\n\nA published archival overview of Kolding's older church books lists the parish priest's register C 469-3 for 1751–1775 and C 469-4 for 1776–1814. The same overview notes a supplementary register of births and baptisms for 1764–1800, held in the parish archive under C 469-74, begun in 1776 under an order of 29 April 1776 (Royal Rentekammer letter of 22 June). This supplementary register may therefore be especially relevant for checking a child born in January 1775 only if its coverage or retrospective entries extend earlier than its stated start; it must not be assumed to include him. The main 1751–1775 register C 469-3 is the direct chronological target for January 1775.\n\nKolding Stadsarkiv confirms that Ingrid Bruun's transcriptions cover Kolding-area church books from 1635 to 1881 and are searchable online. Its broader source-collections page also describes the transcription collection. The transcriptions are a finding aid, not a substitute for checking the original register image. Rigsarkivet's guidance says that pre-1814 parish books are generally chronological *enesteministerialbøger*, so the original search should follow the chronological register for the relevant parish rather than presume a modern tabular birth register.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"What is and is not established","context":"Follow-up: independent catalogue evidence for Kolding's 1775 baptism search (16 November 2026)","text":"- The obituary in Selmer still gives 3 January 1775 in Kolding; Harbou gives 8 January 1775. Neither date has yet been independently checked against the baptism register.\n- The catalogue/overview reference C 469-3 (1751–1775) is a targeted archival pointer, not evidence of the baptism or of either date.\n- C 469-74 is a supplementary 1764–1800 births-and-baptisms list whose described commencement in 1776 means its usefulness for a January 1775 event is uncertain.\n- No new parentage, identity merge, death place or burial claim follows from this catalogue work.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Next action","context":"Follow-up: independent catalogue evidence for Kolding's 1775 baptism search (16 November 2026)","text":"Search Ingrid Bruun's transcription portal for Frederik/Frederich Christian von Kleist and for the parents Christian Frederik af Kleist and Anne Margrethe Schubarth. Then inspect the original Kolding parish register C 469-3 for January 1775 on Arkivalieronline and transcribe the entry exactly, including event date, baptism date, parents, residence and witnesses. If C 469-3 is not the correct parish series, confirm the parish and archival signature with Kolding Stadsarkiv before changing the search target.\n\nSources:\n- Kolding archival overview, “Fortegnelse over Koldings ældre kirkebøger” (downloaded extract): https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/807141.pdf\n- Kolding Stadsarkiv, “Slægtsforskning”: https://koldingstadsarkiv.dk/brug-arkivet/slaegtsforskning\n- Kolding Stadsarkiv, “Kildesamlinger”: https://koldingstadsarkiv.dk/kildesamlinger-2\n- Rigsarkivet, “Kirkebøger – kom godt i gang”: https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/\n- Arkivalieronline, “Kirkebøger fra hele landet”: https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/geo/geo-collection/5\n\n**Assessment:** The direct 1775 target is now narrowed to Kolding parish register C 469-3 (1751–1775), subject to confirmation of the exact parish/archival unit. This is a better-defined search instruction, not a new person-level finding. The 3/8 January discrepancy remains open.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Correction and refinement: the actual Kolding baptism books (16 November 2026)","context":"Correction and refinement: the actual Kolding baptism books (16 November 2026)","text":"The archival signature in the previous follow-up needs correction. Paul G. Ørberg's article “Koldings gamle kirkebøger” in *Vejle Amts Årbog* 1982, pp. 89–101, gives a more detailed inventory than the shorthand used earlier:\n\n- **C 469-3**, the parish priest's church book, covers 1751–1815 and includes baptisms 1751–1815. This is the principal chronological register for the January 1775 baptism.\n- **C 469-7**, the parish clerk's church book, covers 1723–1779 and includes baptisms 1723–1776. This is a second contemporary register that also covers January 1775.\n- **C 469-8**, the parish clerk's church book, covers 1774–1815, but its listed legitimate-baptism section begins in 1780 and illegitimate baptisms in 1777. It is therefore not the direct baptism volume for a legitimate child in January 1775.\n- **C 469-74** is a supplementary list of births and baptisms 1764–1800, begun in 1776. It is not a safe primary target for January 1775.\n\nØrberg explicitly cautions that both church books should be used in historical investigations, since entries, dates, name forms and marginal notes may differ. This makes the comparison of C 469-3 and C 469-7 the correct next step. Rigsarkivet's March 2026 Arkivalieronline update independently lists Kolding Sankt Nikolaj's scanned *enesteministerialbog* content as FVD 1723–1779 and FVD 1774–1815. The latter catalogue grouping should not be confused with Ørberg's older volume-level description of C 469-8's baptism sub-series.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Revised research instruction","context":"Correction and refinement: the actual Kolding baptism books (16 November 2026)","text":"Inspect the January 1775 entries in both the priest's book C 469-3 and clerk's book C 469-7. Search under Frederik/Frederich Christian, but also inspect the entire January sequence because the name may be Latinised, abbreviated or indexed differently. Record exact event/baptism date, parents, residence, witnesses and any marginal annotation. Compare the two entries and then compare their dates with Selmer's 3 January and Harbou's 8 January. Do not infer that the birth date and baptism date are identical.\n\nThis corrects the earlier statement that C 469-3 was limited to 1751–1775: Ørberg lists it as 1751–1815. The 3/8 January discrepancy remains unresolved; no person-level claim or canonical relationship is changed.\n\nSources:\n- Paul G. Ørberg, “Koldings gamle kirkebøger. Et udblik over dansk kirkebogshistorie,” *Vejle Amts Årbog* 1982, pp. 89–101, especially pp. 98–100: https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/807141.pdf\n- Rigsarkivet, “Nyt på Arkivalieronline marts 2026,” pp. 10–11: https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/wp-content/uploads/2022/02/Nyt-fra-de-frivillige-marts-2026.pdf\n- Rigsarkivet, “Kirkebøger – kom godt i gang”: https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/vejledning/kirkeboger-borgerlige-vielser-m-m/kirkeboeger-kom-godt-i-gang/\n\n**Assessment:** The strongest research route is now a two-book comparison of the priest's C 469-3 and clerk's C 469-7. No baptism entry has yet been read, so both dates remain open.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Danish line and date checks","context":"Von Woyten–Kleist descent: supplied pedigree and source audit (16 November 2026)","text":"- The family history identifies Barbara Juliane as daughter of Christian Casimir II.82 and wife of Cartz Ulrik III.369. The existing tree's `p1737` and `p1736` links therefore provide the correct route to Christian Adam `p868`.\n- The 2025 history dates Christian Casimir's death to **19 February 1722**, whereas the current structured data `p3474` says **1 February 1722**. This is a source conflict to resolve, not a date to overwrite without checking the underlying citation.\n- The family history names the wife **Anna Hedwig von Fürst** and gives her death as 1722; the current tree calls her Anna von Fürst. Retain the name variant pending primary-source comparison.\n- The supplied Christian Adam birth date of **3 October 1705** conflicts with the currently used published account and family-association report of **1 October 1705**. The original Vor Frelsers baptism entry has not yet been read; keep 1 October as the current published claim, not as a verified register transcription.\n- The supplied downstream names and dates — Frederik/Friedrich Carl Emil, Christian Frederik Vilhelm, Sophie Frederikke, Julie von Kleist and Eide/Moltke/Rosenkrantz descendants — need their own source chain. They are not established by the Tychow-Dubberow pages audited here. The family history also describes II.82's son Andreas Wilhelm II.117 and his descendants separately; do not conflate that line with Christian Adam's descent through Barbara Juliane.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Nyt officielt søgehjælpemiddel: Kolding Sankt Nikolaj navne-register","context":"Kolding kirkebøger, januar 1775","text":"Rigsarkivets Arkivalieronline har et officielt registreret hjælpemiddel med titlen “Kolding Sankt Nikolaj Sogn: Navneregister til kirkebøger, viede og døde – Kolding (1635–1891)”. Indholdssiden linker til en scannet registerenhed. Det er et nyt konkret søgehjælpemiddel til Kolding-identifikationerne, men sidens titel specificerer ikke tydeligt, om dåbsindførsler fra 1775 er omfattet. Selve registerbilledet kunne ikke læses gennem denne webgrænseflade (AO viser billedviserens ramme, ikke opslagets indhold). Det må derfor ikke registreres som et negativt navnesøg eller som bevis for, at Frederik Christian findes/ikke findes i registret.\n\nNæste kontrol: åbne registeret direkte i Arkivalieronline og afklare dets indhold/alfabetiske dækning; uanset resultat gennemgå de to originale Kolding Sankt Nikolaj-bøger C 469-3 (præstens enesteministerialbog 1751–1815) og C 469-7 (kordegnens bog 1723–1779) omkring januar 1775. Sammenhold derefter dato, forældre, bopæl og faddere med Selmers 3. januar og Harbous 8. januar.","provenance":"Nye webkildekontroller 16.11.2026; Rigsarkivet Arkivalieronline","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"gerda-collateral-parentage":{"person_ids":["p120","p121","p61","g_nchr","g_jchr","g_kand"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"17. Additional Magleby / Tved collateral anchors","context":"17. Additional Magleby / Tved collateral anchors","text":"The evidence-audit compilation also identifies several people with missing parents who sit near the direct South Langeland line. They are not new parent links; they are specific parish targets:\n- **Niels Christiansen** (`g_nchr`), born 24 March 1829 and died 27 April 1912, married Inge Marie Rasmussen (24 September 1830–23 June 1913). Search Magleby baptisms 1829 and 1830 for their parent names.\n- **Jørgen Christensen (Stougaard)** (`g_jchr`) and Karen Andersdatter/Andersen (`g_kand`), both circa 1822, are the proposed parents of candidate Marthe B. Search Magleby and the Stougaard-area parish (possibly Bøstrup or Tranekær) before accepting that connection.\n- **Holger Eriksen** (`p120`) and Anne Cathrine Hansdatter (`p121`) married in Tved on 8 December 1838; their parentage is open. Search Tved and neighbouring parishes.\n- **Karen Nielsen** (`p61`), born circa 1855, has no parents recorded; search Tved baptisms around 1855.\n\nThese are useful next-generation searches, but do not attach any of these people to Kresten or the Langø household based on names alone.","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"gerda-christian-hansen-marriage":{"person_ids":["p114","p115","p57"],"sources":[{"label":"Langø memoir, page 1","url":"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sloev/tree/main/images/langoe-erindringer.jpg"},{"label":"Langø memoir, page 2","url":"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sloev/tree/main/images/langoe-erindringer-s2.jpg"},{"label":"Langeland Byarkiv, register over Lisbeth Skovs artikel “Langø og dæmningen” (Øboer 2011)","url":"https://byarkivet.langelandkommune.dk/boeger-brochurer/register-over-artikler-i-oeboer"},{"label":"Lindelse Nor background","url":"https://da.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lindelse_Nor"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"4. Christian Hansen and Marie Madsdatter","context":"4. Christian Hansen and Marie Madsdatter","text":"Christian Hansen (`p114`) and Marie Madsdatter (`p115`) are ancestors through Maren Christiansen (`p57`). They married in Magleby on 13 November 1829. The marriage references are `LL:13-5235043` and `LL:13-5235044`; the daughter's death/birth-related references are in the ancestor index above. Inspect the original marriage and the children's baptisms to identify their parents and distinguish this household from other Christian Hansen / Marie Madsdatter couples.","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"evald-djursland-collateral":{"person_ids":[],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"11. Additional Djursland collateral and source routes","context":"11. Additional Djursland collateral and source routes","text":"- The wider Djursland dossier proposes Niels Jensen (“Degnen”, 1857–1932) and Karoline Dam Jensen (1863–1947), parents of William Valbjørn (1896–1965) and grandparents of Sigrid Valbjørn (1926). This is a **collateral/unlinked branch** until exact parish entries connect it to a person already in the `p4` ancestry.\n- The Rousing line's strongest record target is the Djurs Nørre herred appointment of Rasmus Jensen Rousing (`p76`) as sognefoged. It may identify residence and patronymic more securely than a name-pattern inference.\n- For the Viby/Syv and Dejret/Rolsøgård leads, negative online searches are already recorded in the legacy archive. The original probate and parish pages are the decisive route; do not repeat broad web searches as though they were new evidence.","provenance":"Tidligere Evald-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"6.2 Djursland — Niels Jensen and Karoline Dam Jensen","context":"6. Branch expansion leads from the expanded master dossier","text":"The dossier proposes Niels Jensen (1857–1932) and Karoline Dam Jensen (1863–1947), and a line to William Valbjørn (1896–1965) and Sigrid Valbjørn (born 15 July 1926). It points to Kolind and Rosmus parish registers and a family poster.\n\n**Next check:** identify the exact baptism, marriage and census entries for each generation. The poster is a navigation aid, not independent evidence.","provenance":"Tværgående forskningsledger, indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"liss-mads-karen-marriage":{"person_ids":["p22","p23"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[]},"lorenz-gedde-officer-audit":{"person_ids":["p108","p216","p217"],"sources":[{"label":"Samuel Christoph Gedde — DBL","url":"https://samlinger.natmus.dk/dmr/asset/193194"},{"label":"Samuel Christoph Gedde — Weilbach","url":"https://weilbach.lex.dk/Samuel_Christoph_Gedde"},{"label":"Christianshavn 1751 map","url":"https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Christianshavn_1751_by_Samuel_Christoph_Gedde.jpg"},{"label":"Christian Giedde — Danish Wikipedia","url":"https://da.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christian_Giedde"},{"label":"Danmarks Adels Aarbog 1919, FamilySearch viewer","url":"`https://www.familysearch.org/library/books/viewer/779881/` (old viewer URL returned 404; catalogue access unverified)"},{"label":"HathiTrust catalogue","url":"https://catalog.hathitrust.org/Record/011408261"},{"label":"Selected DAA volumes at Internet Archive","url":"https://archive.org/details/danmarksadelaarb1900kj"},{"label":"Amtmandsbogen","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/929042.pdf"},{"label":"Mogens Friis — DBL","url":"https://biografiskleksikon.lex.dk/Mogens_Friis"},{"label":"Niels Juel — DBL","url":"https://lex.dk/Niels_Juel"},{"label":"Margrethe Ulfeldt — DBL","url":"https://biografiskleksikon.lex.dk/Margrethe_Ulfeldt"},{"label":"Holger Rosenkrantz portrait, 1636","url":"https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Johan_Thim_1636_Holger_Rosenkrantz_den_laerde_IMG_5857.jpg"},{"label":"Jørgen Holgersen Rosenkrantz portrait, 1640","url":"https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Jørgen_Rosenkrantz.jpg"},{"label":"Niels Juel portrait","url":"https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Niels_Juel_%28Jacob_Coning_painting"},{"label":"Mogens Friis portrait, 1670","url":"https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Mogens_Frijs.jpg"},{"label":"Familien Gedde, 1895","url":"media/familien-gedde-1895.jpg"},{"label":"Harbou, *Slægten von Kleist i Danmark* (1897), pp. 100–102","url":"https://www.v-kleist.com/FG_allg/Kleist_in_Daenemark.pdf"},{"label":"2025 Tychow-Dubberow family history, pp. 165–166","url":"https://www.v-kleist.com/FG/Dubberow.pdf"},{"label":"Vor Frelsers church-book inventory","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/900379.pdf"},{"label":"von Kleist family archive correction on Christian Adam","url":"https://v-kleist.com/FG/Muttrin/fg0283a.htm"},{"label":"Rigsarkivet's church-book release notes (2025)","url":"https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/wp-content/uploads/2022/02/Nyt-fra-de-frivillige-januar-2025.pdf"},{"label":"Schøller, Officersfamilien Gedde (Gjedde)","url":"https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/911651.pdf"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"4. Else Gedde Jensen and the Gedde officer line","context":"4. Else Gedde Jensen and the Gedde officer line","text":"Else (`p13`) was born 31 August 1912 at Ferup, Lejrskov, and baptised 20 October 1912. She was the daughter of Jens Jensen (`p26`) and Paula Mathilde Christle Gedde (`p27`). She received the middle name Gedde through her mother Paula.\n\nPaula's 1895 family photograph is captioned as Edvard Hammer Gedde and Vitta Ludvigsen with their 12 children; child no. 8 is identified as Paula. The caption is family provenance, not a primary birth record. Paula's ancestry connects to Ove Frederik Christopher Gedde (`p108`), Ove Samuel Gedde (`p216`) and Frederikke Louise Dorthea von Kleist (`p217`).\n\nImage: [Familien Gedde, 1895](../images/familien-gedde-1895.jpg).","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"6. Gedde family additions exp001–exp013","context":"6. Gedde family additions exp001–exp013","text":"The previous audit of the expanded research export found:\n- Christian Gedde (1648–1707) and Charlotte von Geidern are identified in DBL as parents of Samuel Christoph Gedde (born 14 July 1691). Other online accounts give different dates for a Christian Gedde namesake; do not merge the identities without checking.\n- Samuel Christoph Gedde (1691–1766) and Marie Elisabeth Pechernaut de la Remière (1705–1751) are reported as parents of ten children, including Hans Christopher and Salomon. Weilbach supports Christian Gedde (1729–1798), cartographer, as another child.\n- Salomon Gedde (born 10 February 1748) married Catharina Elisabeth Qvist in 1784. Catharina's parents are Peter Jacobsen Qvist and Inger, née Ivers; their ancestry remains thinly identified.\n- Frederikke Christiane Fischer and Øllegaard Sophie Fischer are two distinct sisters, not alternate names for one woman.\n- Nicolai Wilhelm Gedde (born 27 December 1779) married Juliane Frederikke Margrethe von Kleist in 1811. DBL gives his death as 31 May 1833, Harbou as 31 August 1833; check Norwegian death/burial records before choosing.\n\nThese are published-genealogy leads. Exact page references and primary parish/military records should be retained alongside each relationship. The full individual audit has been preserved in the research archive; see the index.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Christian Giedde / Christian Gedde — possible namesake conflation","context":"15. Gedde officer-line evidence conflicts and noble collateral ancestry","text":"Two incompatible source descriptions exist for the earlier Christian Gedde/Giedde said to be father of Samuel Christoph Gedde (`p864`):\n\n- The DBL / Harbou-derived source used in the earlier audit identifies **Christian Gedde (1648–1707)**, colonel and commandant on Møn, husband of Charlotte von Geidern, as father of Samuel Christoph (born 14 July 1691).\n- The separate evidence-audit compilation cites Danish Wikipedia for **Christian Giedde (born 1639 in Kville, died 1705)**, husband of Anna Margrethe Charlotte von Geijder (born 1648 on Øsel; daughter of Carl Christian von Geijder and Charlotte von Vietinghoff), and recommends changing p864's father's birth year from 1638 to 1639.\n\nThese cannot be silently merged: the birth/death years and wife's name differ. The 1639 correction may apply to the person currently encoded as p864's father, while the DBL 1648–1707 Møn commandant may be a different Christian or a source conflation. Reconcile the original DBL article, Harbou, Schøller's officer-family genealogy and Møn/Kville records before changing p864's father's dates, spouse or identity. Search Kville baptisms and Møn commandant records. The current 1638 date is not secure; neither is a merge with the 1648–1707 person.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Samuel Christoph Gedde (`p864`)","context":"15. Gedde officer-line evidence conflicts and noble collateral ancestry","text":"The 14 July 1691 Copenhagen birth and 2 February 1766 Copenhagen death are supported by Harbou, DBL and Weilbach. Weilbach also supports his engineering/cartographic career. His 1751 map of Christianshavn is a primary work-product that independently demonstrates his cartographic role. The family history says he and Marie Elisabeth Pechernaut de la Remière had ten children. The exact identity of his father remains subject to the Christian Gedde/Giedde conflict above.\n\nSources:\n- [Samuel Christoph Gedde — DBL](https://samlinger.natmus.dk/dmr/asset/193194)\n- [Samuel Christoph Gedde — Weilbach](https://weilbach.lex.dk/Samuel_Christoph_Gedde)\n- [Christianshavn 1751 map](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Christianshavn_1751_by_Samuel_Christoph_Gedde.jpg)\n- [Christian Giedde — Danish Wikipedia](https://da.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christian_Giedde)","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Hans Christopher Gedde (`p432`) and second-wife spelling/date","context":"15. Gedde officer-line evidence conflicts and noble collateral ancestry","text":"Weilbach and Store norske leksikon give Hans Christopher's exact birth as 23 June 1738 in Oldenburg and death as 9 March 1817 in Copenhagen. The current tree only records the year 1738; the exact date/place is well supported but still secondary-source evidence. Add it only after checking the original Oldenburg baptism.\n\nThe second wife appears as **Øllegaard Sophie Fischer** in Harbou and as **Ødegaard Sophie Fischer** in Anker's 1885 account. Their marriage month also conflicts: Harbou gives 15 February 1789, Anker 15 December 1789. The expanded Gedde research uses 15 December 1789. Preserve both spellings and dates until the Copenhagen marriage and burial entries are inspected.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Rosenkrantz / Krag / Juel / Ulfeldt noble ancestry","context":"15. Gedde officer-line evidence conflicts and noble collateral ancestry","text":"The line above Sophie Rosenkrantz (`p869`) and Christian Rosenkrantz (`p1738`) contains a group of Danish noble families currently sourced mainly through WikiTree/Geni and online biographies. These are secondary compilations, not primary evidence. Danmarks Adels Aarbog (DAA) is the main next cross-check.\n\nSpecific discrepancies and candidates recorded in the evidence audit:\n- **Christian Rosenkrantz til Skovsbo** (`p1738`), reported 1672–3 September 1736; proposed parents Jens Rosenkrantz (1640–1695) and Dorte Friis (1654–1681). Amtmandsbogen and other secondary sources support the family, but verify the DAA Rosenkrantz/Friis entries.\n- **Holger Rosenkrantz “den Lærde”**: birth 2 June 1577 in the current tree conflicts with sources giving 14 December 1574; he is not the same man as **Holger Axelsen Rosenkrantz “den Rige”** (1586–1647). The latter has another date conflict (6 May vs 9 May 1586, likely birth vs baptism). Do not merge the two Holgers.\n- **Sophie Axelsdatter Brahe** (1578–1646) is not the famous Sophia Brahe (1556/1558–1643), sister of Tycho Brahe. They are different people in different branches.\n- **Mogens Friis**: tree date 16 April 1623 conflicts with sources giving 7 September 1623; proposed parents are Niels Friis and Dorthe Mogensdatter Gyldenstjerne. Verify DAA and the original baptism.\n- **Admiral Niels Juel** (1629–1697) is reported as son of Erik Juel (1591–1657) and Sophie Clausdatter Sehested (1594–1658). DBL and other sources support the family.\n- **Margrethe Knudsdatter Ulfeldt** (1641–1703) is reported as daughter of Knud Ulfeldt (1609–1657) and Vibeke Podebusk (1608–1645). DBL supports Margrethe and the Ulfeldt family; DAA should be checked for the full chain.\n- Other names in the proposed noble line include Rosenkrantz, Krag, Juel, Ulfeldt, Podebusk, Friis, Brahe, Gyldenstierne, Høeg and Sehested. Do not add parents or extend these lines based on a name-only match.\n\nUseful source routes:\n- Danmarks Adels Aarbog 1919, FamilySearch viewer (old viewer URL returned 404; catalogue access unverified; original URL: `https://www.familysearch.org/library/books/viewer/779881/`)\n- [HathiTrust catalogue](https://catalog.hathitrust.org/Record/011408261)\n- [Selected DAA volumes at Internet Archive](https://archive.org/details/danmarksadelaarb1900kj)\n- [Amtmandsbogen](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/929042.pdf)\n- [Mogens Friis — DBL](https://biografiskleksikon.lex.dk/Mogens_Friis)\n- [Niels Juel — DBL](https://lex.dk/Niels_Juel)\n- [Margrethe Ulfeldt — DBL](https://biografiskleksikon.lex.dk/Margrethe_Ulfeldt)\n- [Holger Rosenkrantz portrait, 1636](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Johan_Thim_1636_Holger_Rosenkrantz_den_laerde_IMG_5857.jpg)\n- [Jørgen Holgersen Rosenkrantz portrait, 1640](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Jørgen_Rosenkrantz.jpg)\n- [Niels Juel portrait](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Niels_Juel_%28Jacob_Coning_painting%29.jpg)\n- [Mogens Friis portrait, 1670](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Mogens_Frijs.jpg)\n\nNo date or parentage changes from this section have been applied to canonical data.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Næstved / Ludvigsen collateral","context":"16. Broager / Nybøl / Rinkenæs collateral records and name traps","text":"Vitta Dorthea Henriette Mathilde Ludvigsen (`p55`) is in the Gedde family. Her parents Johan Alexander Ludvigsen (`p110`) and Margaretha Dorothea Elisabeth Bølin (`p111`) are named but have little ancestry. Search Næstved Sankt Peders baptisms in 1847 and the parents' marriage; Bølin is a rare surname and should be searched under spelling variants. Keep this as a collateral branch until the marriage and parentage are confirmed.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Kleist line to the Danish Gedde branch: direct chain and next proof targets (16 November 2026)","context":"Kleist line to the Danish Gedde branch: direct chain and next proof targets (16 November 2026)","text":"The latest source comparison makes the Danish connection much clearer. This is the rewarding, well-defined part of the Kleist research and should now take priority over repeating the unresolved Lorenz paternity searches.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"The documented Danish descent","context":"Kleist line to the Danish Gedde branch: direct chain and next proof targets (16 November 2026)","text":"H. W. Harbou's 1897 article, “Slægten von Kleist i Danmark,” gives the following chain:\n1. **Pribislaff III.281** of Muttrin, Borntin, Döbel and Drenow, and Esther von Kameke, were the parents of **Cartz Ulrik III.369**. Harbou records his Brandenburg service in Brabant (1692–1694), Danish service from 9 August 1701, promotion to lieutenant-colonel, death at Rendsburg in 1722 and burial on 17 November.\n2. Cartz Ulrik married **Barbara Juliane von Kleist**, daughter of Christian Casimir II.82 of the Tychow line. This is a marriage linking the Muttrin and Tychow-Dubberow branches of the same wider family.\n3. Their son **Christian Adam III.466** was born 1 October 1705 and baptised at Frelsers Kirke, Christianshavn.\n4. Christian Adam's son **Christian Frederik III.601** was born 25 August 1743. He married Anna Margrethe Schubart in 1770.\n5. Their daughter **Frederikke Louise Dorthea** was born 5 January 1786 and married **Ove Samuel Gedde** on 24 June 1806.\n\nThis is the explicit published bridge from the Kleist family into the Danish Gedde line. Harbou's article cites military, church, probate and official records in its notes, but remains a 1897 synthesis; the cited original records should be checked before describing every link as primary-record proven. The key names, dates and relationship statements are recorded in Harbou, pp. 95–102. Source: https://www.v-kleist.com/FG_allg/Kleist_in_Daenemark.pdf.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Where to verify it in the archives","context":"Kleist line to the Danish Gedde branch: direct chain and next proof targets (16 November 2026)","text":"The family association's Greifswald archive inventory gives the most actionable targets:\n- **Landesarchiv Greifswald, Rep. 40 Nr. 35 gg, Bl. 2–13:** 99 extracts of Kleist fief documents assembled by Lehnsarchivar Thilo. The family association notes that the original was sold to Peter Christian von Kleist in 1766 and that it is uncertain whether the text now in the archive is the original or a later copy.\n- The same file includes genealogical work by Pastor Steinbrück (father, c.1780) and his son (c.1820–1830). These are family-history compilations, not contemporary records.\n- **Rep. 41 Nr. 28 Plathe:** the two versions of von der Osten's Adelsspiegel, with handwritten additions. Useful as a separate comparison witness, but not a substitute for the fief documents.\n\nThe family association's archive inventory is a secondary finding aid, but it gives precise shelfmarks and describes the provenance caveats: https://www.v-kleist.com/bestand_greifswald.htm. It also identifies Rep. 40 III 35ac and Rep. 40 III 35gg as Steinbrück material. A request should ask staff to identify all extracts concerning III.22, III.54, III.114, III.186, III.281 and III.369, and to distinguish the original charter, contemporary copy, later extract and genealogical annotation.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Keep the early-medieval uncertainty in its proper place","context":"Kleist line to the Danish Gedde branch: direct chain and next proof targets (16 November 2026)","text":"The 1477 charter conflict over whether Reimer and Bartes were sons of Pribbeslaw of Muttrin or Hans of Tychow concerns the collateral Tychow branch. It does not erase Harbou's later documented marriage link between Barbara Juliane of Tychow and Cartz Ulrik of Muttrin, nor the explicit Danish descent through Christian Adam, Christian Frederik and Frederikke Louise. It does, however, mean that the early Tychow pedigree must remain qualified. The 1364 Prissebur record, the 1477 collective fief letter and the later family genealogies are separate pieces of evidence; none alone proves an unbroken descent from the 13th century to Cartz Ulrik.\n\n**Working conclusion:** the Kleist-to-Gedde connection through Cartz Ulrik and Frederikke Louise Dorthea is a credible, specifically documented published genealogy. The immediate parentage of Cartz Ulrik is supported by Harbou and the family register; the generations behind Pribislaff III.281 are supported by a denser but still secondary property/genealogy trail. The next high-value action is to retrieve the fief extracts and inheritance documents at Greifswald, then compare Harbou's Danish citations with the original church and probate records. No canonical parent edge is changed by this review.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Danish-side parish records: exact catalogue targets (16 November 2026)","context":"Kleist line to the Danish Gedde branch: direct chain and next proof targets (16 November 2026)","text":"Harbou's Danish branch can now be checked against named church-book series rather than only the printed genealogy:\n\n- **Christian Adam III.466, born 1 October 1705:** Harbou says he was baptised at Vor Frelsers Kirke, Christianshavn. Rigsarkivet's 2025 release notes identify a Vor Frelsers name index to the bell-ringer's single church register, covering births 1704–1822. A separate inventory of the church books says the index for 1704–1822 is book 20 and points to the bell-ringer's baptism registers in books 15–19 and 36. Use the index to locate Christian Adam, then inspect the referenced baptism entry; the index alone is not proof of parentage. Sources: Rigsarkivet, *Nyt på Arkivalieronline, november 2025*, p. 10, https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/wp-content/uploads/2022/02/Nyt-fra-de-frivillige-november-2025.pdf; *Vor Frelsers Sogn*, church-book inventory, https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/900379.pdf.\n- **Christian Frederik III.601, born 25 August 1743 in Rendsburg:** two plausible parish targets have surviving records for 1743. Rendsburg Christkirche lists baptisms 1741–1765 and a baptism name register 1700–1749; Rendsburg St. Marien lists baptisms 1739–1800 and name registers 1701–1749 / 1700–1799. Since Harbou gives only the town, not the church, check both indexes before concluding that an entry is missing. Sources: Archion, Rendsburg Christkirche, https://www.archion.de/de/alle-archive/schleswig-holstein/landeskirchliches-archiv-der-evangelisch-lutherischen-kirche-in-norddeutschland/kirchenkreis-rendsburg-eckernfoerde/rendsburg-christkirche/298973; Archion, Rendsburg St. Marien, https://www.archion.de/de/alle-archive/schleswig-holstein/landeskirchliches-archiv-der-evangelisch-lutherischen-kirche-in-norddeutschland/kirchenkreis-rendsburg-eckernfoerde/rendsburg-st-marien.\n- **Christian Frederik's marriage to Anna Margrethe Schubart, 3 December 1770, and daughter Frederikke Louise Dorthea, born 5 January 1786:** Bredstedt is a sensible first parish to check because Harbou says Christian Frederik had an estate near Bredstedt and died there in 1799, but the article does not explicitly state that either event occurred in Bredstedt. The Bredstedt church-book overview lists a mixed book with baptisms 1764–1830 and marriages 1764–1826 (book 13); Archion lists the corresponding baptism and combined marriage volumes. Check Bredstedt first, then broaden to neighbouring parishes if the entries are absent. Sources: Landeskirchliches Archiv, *Kirchenbuchübersicht 2 Nordfriesland*, Bredstedt, https://www.archiv-nordkirche.de/files/landeskirchliches_archiv/Kirchenbuchuebersichten/Kirchenbuch%C3%BCbersichten/Kirchenbuchuebersicht_2_Nordfriesland.pdf; Archion Bredstedt, https://www.archion.de/de/alle-archive/schleswig-holstein/landeskirchliches-archiv-der-evangelisch-lutherischen-kirche-in-norddeutschland/kirchenkreis-nordfriesland/bredstedt/263498.\n\nThese catalogue checks establish that the target series exist and where to look; none of the individual entries has yet been read. The efficient order is: Vor Frelsers index → Christian Adam's baptism; Rendsburg St. Marien and Christkirche indexes → Christian Frederik's baptism; Bredstedt marriage and baptism registers → the 1770 marriage and Frederikke's birth. If an Archion login is unavailable, the Kirchenkreis archives accept research enquiries and can provide copies for a fee.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Kleist–Gedde line: source-critical update (16 November 2026)","context":"Kleist–Gedde line: source-critical update (16 November 2026)","text":"Harbou's 1897 account, pp. 100–102, explicitly places Christian Adam III.466 as the son of Cartz Ulrik III.369 and Barbara Juliane von Kleist, and identifies Cartz Ulrik as son of Pribislaff III.281 and Esther von Kameke. Harbou also flags the older *Dansk biografisk Lexikon* IX, p. 219, error that makes Christian Adam the son of his maternal grandfather Christian Casimir. Christian Casimir was Barbara Juliane's father and Christian Adam's maternal grandfather, not his father. Do not restore that mistaken parent edge.\n\nThe name should be normalized as **Christian Adam**, while retaining **Christian Adrian** as a historical variant in older German family literature. The von Kleist family archive's online correction reports that a Danish archival investigation established the 1 October 1705 birth date and the Adam form; this remains a reported archival finding until the underlying register entry is inspected.\n\n**Primary-record target — Vor Frelsers, Christianshavn:** Harbou reports baptism on 1 October 1705. Search the parish minister's register FVD 1686–1722 around 1–3 October 1705 (the alternative 3 October date occurs in the older biographical account). Also request the bell-ringer's parallel baptism index, volume 20 (birth index 1704–1822), and follow its reference into the bell-ringer's baptism books, volumes 15–19 or 36. The index/catalogue identifies a search route; it is not itself proof of the parentage. The decisive check is the original baptism entry naming the parents.\n\n**Independent identification of Frederikke:** the Fredensborg Slotssogn baptism of Ove Frederik Christopher Gedde, 24 July 1808 (BSID 594366, image 65), records his parents as Ove Samuel Gedde and “Frederikke Lovise Birthe født Kleist”. Harbou (1897) calls Ove Samuel's wife Frederikke Louise Dorthea. The matching husband, surname and chronology strongly support that this is the same wife, but preserve Birthe/Dorthea as a source variant until the original image and marriage/baptism records are compared. This baptism independently supports her identity as a née Kleist; it does not independently prove her parents.\n\nHarbou gives Christian Frederik III.601's birth as 25 August 1743, his marriage to Anna Margrethe Schubart on 3 December 1770, and daughter Frederikke's birth as 5 January 1786. Rendsburg St. Marien and Christkirche are both candidate baptism series for 1743; neither parish is yet confirmed. Bredstedt is only a first search hypothesis for the 1770 marriage and 1786 baptism, not an established event locality. No individual entry for these events has yet been inspected.\n\n**Current assessment:** the published descent Cartz Ulrik → Christian Adam → Christian Frederik → Frederikke → Ove Samuel Gedde is a credible, well-defined line for public display with an explicit “needs more evidence” status at the primary-register level. The 1808 baptism provides independent evidence for Ove Samuel's wife being née Kleist, but not for her parentage. No canonical relationship or confidence field is changed by this note.\n\nSources: [Harbou, *Slægten von Kleist i Danmark* (1897), pp. 100–102](https://www.v-kleist.com/FG_allg/Kleist_in_Daenemark.pdf); [2025 Tychow-Dubberow family history, pp. 165–166](https://www.v-kleist.com/FG/Dubberow.pdf); [Vor Frelsers church-book inventory](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/900379.pdf); [von Kleist family archive correction on Christian Adam](https://v-kleist.com/FG/Muttrin/fg0283a.htm); [Rigsarkivet's church-book release notes (2025)](https://www.rigsarkivet.dk/wp-content/uploads/2022/02/Nyt-fra-de-frivillige-januar-2025.pdf).","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"5. Gedde / Gjedde line","context":"5. Gedde / Gjedde line","text":"Research leads identify Ove Gjedde (1594–1660), son of Brostrup Gjedde and Dorthe Ulfeldt, and Brostrup Gjedde (c. 1560–1614). The expanded dossier also proposes Ove Samuel Gedde and Frederikke Louise Dorthea von Kleist as a connection between the officer families.\n\n**Next check:** verify the exact people, marriage/union and children in C. E. Schøller's *Stamtavle over Officersfamilien Gedde (Gjedde)*, then corroborate with military rolls and contemporary parish or probate records. Do not attach them to the current branch on surname or association alone.\n\nReference supplied: [Schøller, Officersfamilien Gedde (Gjedde)](https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/911651.pdf).","provenance":"Tværgående forskningsledger, indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-eide-identity":{"person_ids":[],"sources":[{"label":"NMBU-afhandling om Frederik Carl Eide","url":"https://nmbu.brage.unit.no/nmbu-xmlui/bitstream/handle/11250/2648017/mif35.pdf?isAllowed=y&sequence=1"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"Frederik Carl Eide: a separate identity investigation","context":"Additional reverse pedigree: 19th-century Danish side branches and medieval claims (16 November 2026)","text":"The supplied claim that Frederik Carl Eide (born 2 March 1815 in Christiania) was an illegitimate son of the later Christian VIII and Johanna Maria Christensdatter Bandvold needs careful handling. A modern Norwegian academic thesis reports that the infant Frederik Carl was born to Marie/Johanna Maria Brandvold in March 1815 and was brought into her subsequent marriage to Lars Larsen Eide in April 1815; the child thereafter used the Eide surname. The source is useful for the mother's marriage and child's social/legal upbringing, but the excerpt located so far does not establish the alleged royal paternity. Treat the paternity as an unverified claim unless supported by contemporary correspondence, acknowledged paternity or a reliable biographical source. Do not state it as fact in the tree.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"liss-pedersen-vester-starup":{"person_ids":["p10","p11"],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"2. Pedersen / Vester Starup line","context":"2. Pedersen / Vester Starup line","text":"Jens Nielsen Pedersen (`p10`) was born 14 April 1891 in Vester Starup and married Hulda Christensen on 11 June 1916 in Ringkøbing. At the 1925 census the family was in Silkeborg with daughters Karen Margrethe, Anna Elvira and Nelly; Liss was born there in 1931. His father Niels Pedersen (`p20`) was born 25 October 1861 in Vester Starup and married Ane Else Pedersen in 1889. The 1860 Vester Starup marriage of Jens Pedersen (`p40`) names his father as Peder Jensen and the bride Kirsten Marie Nielsen's father as Niels Jepsen. These parent references are recorded in the canonical tree but the earlier generation identities still need parish-record corroboration.","provenance":"Tidligere Liss-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"liss-west-jutland-collateral":{"person_ids":["as_p11_1","as_p11_6"],"sources":[{"label":"Horsbøl genealogical collection","url":"https://www.horsboel.dk/familie/grind/dat16.htm"},{"label":"1870 census, Sønder Omme","url":"https://www.horsboel.dk/fkt/FKT_Sdr_Omme_1870.pdf"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"6. Other unlinked West Jutland material","context":"6. Other unlinked West Jutland material","text":"The old branch dossier also mentions the broader Assing, Sønder Felding, Sønder Omme and Pårup Melgaard area. Only the Jens Peder Larsen → Kjersten Frederiksdatter → Karen Jensen branch is presently tied to this grandparent's direct ancestry in the canonical data. Any additional Larsen or local families must remain collateral/unlinked until matched by exact parish, spouse and child records.","provenance":"Tidligere Liss-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"6.1 West Jutland — Pårup Melgaard","context":"6. Branch expansion leads from the expanded master dossier","text":"The dossier proposes Jens Peder Larsen, born 29 February 1812 in Assing, died 1870 in Sønder Omme, married Mette Pedersdatter in Sønder Felding in 1843. It also flags foster children and collateral non-marital connections in the heath parishes.\n\nReferences supplied:\n- [Horsbøl genealogical collection](https://www.horsboel.dk/familie/grind/dat16.htm)\n- [1870 census, Sønder Omme](https://www.horsboel.dk/fkt/FKT_Sdr_Omme_1870.pdf)\n\n**Next check:** inspect original birth, marriage and death entries. For each *plejebarn*, record the exact census wording and household relationship. A census household listing supports a household/foster connection, not biological parentage by itself.","provenance":"Tværgående forskningsledger, indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"gerda-south-langeland-sources":{"person_ids":[],"sources":[{"label":"Elgezira local history","url":"https://elgezira.dk/historie.html"},{"label":"Maternal-side poster","url":"https://sloev.github.io/tree/plakat-A3-mors-side.pdf"}],"research_notes":[{"title":"8. Other South Langeland leads","context":"8. Other South Langeland leads","text":"- The family material mentions a one-copy Sydlangeland death list for 1900–1970; preserve a scan and record its exact bibliographic/provenance details before relying on it.\n- The older dossier mentions Christian Hansen and Marie Madsdatter as a Magleby couple; this is now represented by `p114`/`p115`, but the original marriage should be reread.\n- The family notes mention relatives from South Langeland emigrating to America. Keep each person and destination separate; do not merge the Jørgen/Bodil family group with other emigrants.","provenance":"Tidligere Gerda-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"6.3 South Langeland — Christian Hansen and the Langø line","context":"6. Branch expansion leads from the expanded master dossier","text":"The dossier describes a coastal farming and maritime branch around Nordenbro and Magleby, proposes a marriage in Magleby in 1829, and points to local-history material and a Nordenbro probate record from 1787.\n\nReferences supplied:\n- [Elgezira local history](https://elgezira.dk/historie.html)\n- [Maternal-side poster](https://sloev.github.io/tree/plakat-A3-mors-side.pdf)\n\nA related tree export names Christian Hansen (born c. 1806) and Marie Madsdatter (born c. 1807), married 13 November 1829 in Magleby, but supplies no parents or exact register image.\n\n**Next check:** find the marriage entry, transcribe witnesses, residences and ages, then follow each spouse through baptism, census and probate sources. Establish whether this couple belongs to the Langø line or a collateral family before connecting them.","provenance":"Tværgående forskningsledger, indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"lorenz-broager-nybol-records":{"person_ids":[],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"16. Broager / Nybøl / Rinkenæs collateral records and name traps","context":"16. Broager / Nybøl / Rinkenæs collateral records and name traps","text":"The broader extension contains a set of unlinked or collateral Sønderjylland families. Keep them separate from Lorenz's paternity question unless a record explicitly joins them:\n\n- **Peter Christian Frederiksen** (`p48`) is described as son of Friedrich Friedrichsen (`p96`) and Anna Christina Peters (`p97`), married in Broager on 9 December 1860. His son Lorenz Heinrich Frederiksen (`p24`) is an **older namesake**, not this grandparent (`p6`).\n- **Anna Kirstine Marie Paulsen** (`p49`) is described as daughter of Jens Paulsen (`p98`) and Cathrine Marie Lausen/Lorenzen (`p99`), married in Ulkebøl on 24 February 1833.\n- **Carl Peter Hansen** (`p50`) and Eline Helene Marie Hansen (`p51`) are parents of Cathrina Maria Magdalena Hansen (`p25`); Peter Hansen (`p100`) is described as a teglværksarbejder in Egernsund and Anna Christina Jensen (`p101`) as born in Gudum.\n- The four relevant marriage entries are cited as `LL:13-4957004`, `LL:13-4957007` and `LL:13-4995190`; read them in full because they reportedly name parents' parishes. Then inspect Broager, Ulkebøl, Egernsund and Nybøl baptisms around 1799–1850. Confirm the places “Vester Hostrup”, “Brejning” and “Gudum” against the original entries; they currently derive partly from census/transcription.\n- Egernsund teglværk personnel protocols at Broagerlands Lokalarkiv may identify teglværksarbejdere. For events after 1867, use German Standesamt registers at Kreisarchiv Schleswig-Flensburg, not Danish church books alone.\n\n**Rinkenæs / Dalsgård:** Jens Jensen (`p26`) died 26 March 1951 at Dalsgård; Paula Gedde (`p27`) died 29 February 1952 there. Their burials in Rinkenæs and the Dalsgård deed are open checks. The seven children are listed in the tree; the extension notes Mathilde (`s_p26_4`) was unmarried and one child (`s_p26_0`) died in infancy. The farm deed may clarify the household and property history, not Lorenz's biological father.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Review of the supplied cross-branch research ledger (16 November 2026)","context":"16. Broager / Nybøl / Rinkenæs collateral records and name traps","text":"The newly supplied `lead/tree-research.md` has been compared with the active dossiers. Most of its West Jutland, Djursland, South Langeland and Lorenz paternity material was already represented in the relevant active dossier and does not provide new primary evidence. The following additional detail is retained here because it sharpens the von Woyten / von Stojentin / von Kleist research plan.\n\n**Christina von Woyten — dates absent; possible successive marriages / namesake issue:** the 2025 revised German *Tychow-Dubberower Linie* (Familienverband derer v. Kleist, https://www.v-kleist.com/FG/Dubberow.pdf) gives two relevant biographies. On p. 143 (II.55), Georg von Kleist of Groß-Tychow (died after 1645) is said to have married Christina von Woyten, widow of Otto von Below of Pustamin, daughter of Benedictus von Woyten of Versin and Elisabeth von Stojentin of Giesebitz; Georg's only daughter was Hedwig Maria. This supports the current Christina–Georg II.55–Hedwig Maria unit. On p. 36 (II.44), a different Georg von Kleist of Crolow (died 1629) is said, citing von der Osten, to have married a Christina Woyten of Versin who bore him no children, and then Dorothea von Below, who bore two sons. These are two different Georgs. The two Christina references may describe one woman in successive marriages (Otto von Below → Georg II.44 → Georg II.55) or two women with the same name and family origin; the source does not resolve this. Keep this as an explicit identity question, not a settled chronology. **No birth/baptism or death/burial date for Christina is supplied in either passage or the currently cited Harbou genealogy**, so both date fields must remain blank. Her father's being documented after 6 December 1584 does not itself date her. Do not estimate her dates from relatives' lifespans or create a second Christina node without distinguishing evidence.\n\n**Earlier von Woyten ancestry — unverified Zedler lead:** the supplied ledger proposes Michael von Woyten (ensign in Charles V's service) → Conrad von Woyten → Conrad Pantaleon von Woyten → Vandemar von Woyten → Carl Woiten (flourished c. 1400), and names Martin von Woyten as Benedictus's great-uncle. The supplied material gives no precise Zedler page/column or underlying source. Treat every generation as a separate research lead, not as an established chain. Next inspect *Grosses vollständiges Universal-Lexicon*, vol. 59, article “Woyden, Woyten”, capture exact page/column and wording, then follow its citations.\n\n**Further Stojentin hypothesis:** the ledger suggests Hans (Johann) von Stojentin (1446–1517), lord of Gohren and Landvogt of Stolp, as a possible father of Elisabeth von Stojentin of Giesebitz; it further proposes Peter von Stojentin (c. 1400–1470) and Elisabeth von Lettow as Hans's parents. This remains unproven. An estate association or shared family name is not parentage evidence.\n\n**Early Muttrin / medieval von Kleist names:** the ledger also lists Klest de Densin (mentioned 1289), Prissebur (1364), Jarßlaff and sons (reportedly mentioned 1407), Henning (alive after 1435), and Bisprow (enfeoffed 1477, died c. 1493). These are navigation clues into the Muttrin-Damen material, not a demonstrated continuous line to the canonical people. The existing exp020–exp042 cautions remain controlling: distinguish contemporary charter statements from later family-genealogy reconstructions, keep numbering systems separate, and do not bridge generations without the cited document.\n\n**Gjedde versus Gedde:** the supplied ledger names admiral Ove Gjedde (1594–1660), son of Brostrup Gjedde and Dorthe Ulfeldt, and Brostrup Gjedde (c. 1560–1614). This is a separate historical line unless evidence establishes a connection to the later officer-family Gedde. The next secondary-source cross-check is C. E. Schøller, *Stamtavle over Officersfamilien Gedde (Gjedde)*, followed by military rolls and contemporary parish/probate evidence. Do not attach Ove or Brostrup Gjedde to the current Gedde branch on surname resemblance.\n\nThe ledger's remaining material was checked against the active dossiers:\n- The Pårup Melgaard / Assing spouse conflict (Mette Pedersdatter versus Kjersten Frederiksdatter) is already recorded in the Liss dossier; Kjersten remains the working identification pending the original 1843 marriage.\n- Niels Jensen “Degnen” and Karoline Dam Jensen remain explicitly collateral/unlinked in the Evald dossier.\n- Christian Hansen and Marie Madsdatter, and the unlinked 1787 Nordenbro probate, are already recorded in the Gerda dossier.\n- The Lorenz parentage distinctions between Sophus Petersen (family-claimed biological father), Wilhelm Krogh (declaration/civil registration) and Peter Frederiksen (step/social father) are already recorded here. The new ledger does not supply new evidence resolving the dispute.\n\nThe original supplied ledger has been preserved as `research-archive/tree-research-ledger-2026-11-16.md` for provenance. It is an archived source of research leads, not a second active dossier.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Detlef Krogh in the 1851 Nybøl property list","context":"Adelby baptism route and Nybøl holding no. 32: new source checks (16 November 2026)","text":"The local-history booklet *Fra Nybøl sogn*, no. 3 (1981), contains a list titled “Fortegnelse over bol, kådnere og inderste i Nybøl 1851” (contents page 71; printed list on p. 71). Under “Kådnere - Husmandssteder” it lists “32. Detlef Krogh - Fra 1883 kun indersted.” The same list separately labels the next category “Inderste - Husejere.” The booklet explains that the list is keyed to a town map and that some numbered sites had been moved or had earlier uses. This is a specific place/holding lead for a Detlef Krogh in Nybøl, not a birth, family, or identity record. The phrase “Fra 1883 kun indersted” appears to describe a change in the status/use of the site from 1883; it must not be read as proof that Detlef himself lived there continuously from 1851 to 1883, nor as a date of birth or death. No link has yet been demonstrated between this Detlef and the proposed Wilhelm father Detlef Krogh (b. 1876), the proposed brother Detlef (b. 1905), or the older Detlef Krogh senior (c. 1849–1931). The property number and map are useful targets for land, census, and local address cross-checking.\n\nSource: Peter Petersen, “Fortegnelse over bol, kådnere og inderste i Nybøl 1851,” in *Fra Nybøl sogn*, no. 3 (Hjemstavnsforeningen for Nybøl Sogn, 1981), p. 71; digitised copy at https://slaegtsbibliotek.dk/2025/953243.pdf (PDF printed page 71; extracted text lines 2142–2193). The publication's contents and introductory note identify this as a 1981 local-history volume and describe the register as map-based.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Adelby 1908 baptism: catalogue discrepancy and access route","context":"Adelby baptism route and Nybøl holding no. 32: new source checks (16 November 2026)","text":"The Landeskirchliches Archiv's Adelby church-book overview describes a baptism register “Taufen 1882–1949,” book no. 2.3, and specifically notes entries concerning the naming of illegitimate children. This is the right register series to ask about for a claimed Wilhelm Krogh birth on 9 October 1908. However, the currently visible Archion catalogue entry for Adelby “Taufen 1882–1907” (local signature Adelby_2.3) only advertises digitised coverage through 1907; it does not expose a 1908 volume/entry in the public catalogue. The register overview and online catalogue therefore do not align on the displayed end date. I have not inspected the original 1908 entry and cannot yet confirm whether 9 October is a birth date or baptism date, or whether the parents are recorded as Detlef Krogh and Kirstine Marie Jensen.\n\nSources:\n- Landeskirchliches Archiv, *Kirchenbuchübersicht 1 Schleswig-Flensburg*, Adelby entry: https://www.archiv-nordkirche.de/files/landeskirchliches_archiv/Kirchenbuchuebersichten/Kirchenbuch%C3%BCbersichten/Kirchenbuchuebersicht_1_Schleswig-Flensburg.pdf\n- Archion, Adelby, “Taufen 1882–1907,” local signature Adelby_2.3: https://www.archion.de/de/alle-archive/schleswig-holstein/landeskirchliches-archiv-der-evangelisch-lutherischen-kirche-in-norddeutschland/kirchenkreis-schleswig-flensburg/adelby/273637\n\nNext record action: ask the Landeskirchliches Archiv whether the 1908–1949 continuation of Adelby baptism book 2.3 survives and whether it can be consulted or supplied as a copy; if accessible, inspect the full entry and any marginal notes, then search the Adelby marriage register 1884–1937 for Detlef Krogh and Kirstine Marie Jensen. Archion's public catalogue lists the marriage register for 1884–1937 (local signature Adelby_5.3), but the entry has not been searched. Do not treat the proposed parent couple or Wilhelm's 1908 date as verified until those records are read.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"},{"title":"Status","context":"Adelby baptism route and Nybøl holding no. 32: new source checks (16 November 2026)","text":"The Nybøl property list is a useful new independent local-history lead but does not resolve the Detlef identities. The Adelby baptism series is located, but the relevant 1908 portion is not currently exposed in the online catalogue. The next priority remains obtaining the 1908 baptism entry, followed by a marriage/household check for the proposed parents and a map/property-number identification for Nybøl holding 32.","provenance":"Tidligere Lorenz-dossier, fuldt indarbejdet 16.11.2026","imported_on":"2026-11-16"}]},"evald-open-archive-checks":{"person_ids":[],"sources":[],"research_notes":[{"title":"7. Open tasks in priority order","context":"7. Open tasks in priority order","text":"1. Obtain the original 1764 Rolsøgård probate for Christen Christensen “Tohde?” in Dejret; identify all stepchildren and the biological parent(s).\n2.
